# W2-03 code-anchored build spec

Decision `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-03-cold-assessment.md` on the registered W2-02 replay. Use only accepted v4 public evidence, effective configuration and clock/source attestation. The six W2-02 manifest cases are the required phase-1 input; do not hand-insert accepted rows.

## Inputs, model and selection

Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold.py` with frozen `ColdEvidence` (`evidence_id`, `envelope_sha256`, `family`, `record_kind`, `role`, `asset_id`, `source_time`, `received_at`, `decided_at`, `quality`, `value`, `unit`, `event_id`, `correlation_id`, `outcome`, `source_id`, `clock_attested`), `ColdWindow` (`from_at`,`to_at`,`known_at`), `ColdExcursion`, `ColdStartChain` and `ColdAssessment`. Add pure `assess_excursion` and `assess_start_response`; IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:query_evidence,_configuration,_persist,enqueue` is the pattern: select `accepted_evidence` joined to `ingestion_batch`, time-effective `foundation_revision` and `binding_set_revision` via `basis_from_snapshot`, and S4-05 `batch_publication_link/evidence_publication` for provenance. Require `site_id`, `source_time >= from`, `< to`, `received_at <= known_at`, and ingestion `decided_at <= known_at`. Order `(source_time,received_at,id)`. Only exact `cold-roles-1` effective row/asset/role, GOOD, calibrated primary chamber probe and attested clock is qualifying; SUSPECT, unbound, clock-skewed and contradictory records remain linked as CONTEXT/EXCLUDED with reason. Do not choose a latest publication by display name. Select the effective Foundation and binding at each evidence source time, and refuse if multiple periods overlap. `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark at request start; never a simulated clock. Basis SHA is SHA-256 of canonical JSON with keys `analytic_id,model_version,tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,source_gate,records`; records sorted by `(evidence_id,envelope_sha256)` and include both fields. `source_gate` is the exact S4-05 classification plus source IDs, not a boolean.

`assessment_worker.py:DispatchAssessmentWorker` is the orchestration pattern. Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` with `assess_explicit` and `run_once`, using `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence`; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts/reads `finding` and `finding_revision`. Refactor both adapters to accept one caller-owned SQLAlchemy `Connection`; worker starts one transaction, persists assessment, episode, Finding and completes the job in it. No partial projection on failure. Existing dispatch methods keep their old transactions and bytes. Duplicate basis returns the existing revision; changed basis appends a revision. Relevant cold `accepted_evidence` after intake enqueues a bracketed cold job; duplicate or rejected ordinal does not. A queued/failed newer job makes previous selected read `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until the shared transaction commits.

## Rules and exact outcomes

Expected points are the 13 five-minute instants 19:00 through 20:00 in `[19:00,20:05)`; coverage numerator is distinct qualifying probe timestamps, denominator 13 (general windows derive their own aligned count), capped at 1. Need `>=0.8`, with no inferred path across a >10-minute gap. Above upper or below lower policy needs two consecutive GOOD samples at least five minutes apart; return needs two consecutive values inside band by at least effective `cold-return-hysteresis`. Start bounds `[last in-band,first out-of-band]`, end bounds `[last out-of-band,first stable in-band]`; initially out of band means unknown earliest start. Open episode has `duration_bounds.high=null`. Low duration is max(0, earliest return - latest start), or window end - latest start for a continuously observed open episode; high is latest return - earliest start only when both known. `degree_minute_bounds` is the sum of adjacent observed rectangles over intervals <=10 minutes using min/max exceedance; no gap area. `peak_observed` is max observed sample. Exposure Finding requires 15-minute low duration, >=0.8 coverage and source gate; otherwise POSSIBLE/UNKNOWN. No spoilage, part-failure or continuous curve claim. Normal -> `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`. Excursion -> first high at 19:15, open, duration low 45 min, high null. Backup-failed -> same exposure, door CONTEXT. Missing-probe -> 7/13, temperature UNKNOWN and no exposure Finding. Late-reading -> 12/13 at 20:05 and 13/13 at 20:10 with new basis/revision, old history retained.

Start chain groups by `(correlation_id,generator_asset_id,outage episode)` and accepted event IDs. Required steps: POWER_LOSS, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, generator state, TRANSFER_RESULT if sensor bound, bus state, compressor state, probe response. Within 30-second attested source-clock ordering tolerance, request 19:05 -> success 19:07 -> transfer 19:08 for normal. Explicit failed result 19:05 after 19:00 request supports `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with no conflicting GOOD success. Timeout from Foundation = 5 min; inferred failure from timeout additionally requires complete generator state coverage through timeout. Missing transfer is `UNKNOWN_STEP` for transfer only; bus state cannot replace it. Contradictory GOOD success and failure for same correlation -> `CONTRADICTORY`, never failure. Equal event-ID conflict is a rejected S2 ordinal, not an accepted contradictory outcome. Excluded clock skew/SUSPECT/unbound records never support claims. Claim levels are `OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY`.

Episode key is SHA-256 canonical JSON array `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,first_qualifying_event_or_crossing_evidence_id]` (UUIDs lowercase text), stored as `episode_key_sha256`. Stable anchor survives late evidence changing bounds. A newly supported different anchor after old invalidation creates a new episode and `split_from_episode_id`; old episode gets an immutable `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` revision and FindingRevision. Other transitions are `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked evidence changes and `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration/source gate/known cutoff changes. Excursion and start response have separate Findings, can share public events, and later use one overlap group. Reuse `postgres_finding_store.py:_insert_finding,_insert_revision` with domain `cold_chain`; avoid dispatch-specific candidate-minute fields.

## Migration `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py`

After `0021`, create `cold_episode(id uuid PK,tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,site_id uuid NOT NULL,kind text CHECK IN ('EXCURSION','START_RESPONSE'),asset_id uuid NOT NULL,anchor_time timestamptz NOT NULL,episode_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,split_from_episode_id uuid NULL,finding_id uuid NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)`; scoped composite FKs to Site, asset, optional split episode and Finding. `finding_id` stays null in this append-only slice: Finding links to episode through its immutable `claim.episode_id` and exact episode revision; no post-insert episode UPDATE fills the optional reverse pointer. Index `(site_id,kind,anchor_time)`. Unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`; scoped FKs to episode and assessment revision. Check each earliest/latest pair ordered when both present, low/high numeric pairs nonnegative and ordered when both present, JSON array shape, and peak null if no usable point. Index `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)`. Create `cold_episode_evidence(episode_revision_id uuid,tenant_id uuid,site_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,use text CHECK IN ('SUPPORT','CONTEXT','EXCLUDED'),reason text NULL,PRIMARY KEY(episode_revision_id,evidence_id,use))` with scoped FKs to episode revision and accepted evidence. Install append-only UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers on all three; no upsert update.

Widen `finding` and `finding_revision` from `0009_finding_store.py`: `finding.subject_asset_id uuid NULL` scoped asset FK; existing `generator_asset_id` is **text**, so keep its type and only drop NOT NULL. Add domain CHECK `mini_grid` requires generator nonnull and subject null; `cold_chain` requires subject nonnull and generator null. Add unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)`; `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL` backfill old rows `mini_grid`, remove temporary default, replace parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` and preserve old mini-grid checks. For `cold_chain`, `episode_start/end`, `candidate_minutes`, `necessary_minutes`, `indeterminate_minutes` nullable; all three minute fields null. Replace bound CHECK with null-or-ordered and a separate mini-grid both-nonnull CHECK. Keep existing transition CHECK, provenance and source columns. `recompute_job.domain` is text and needs no SQL widening. Downgrade refuses if any cold episode/revision/link or cold Finding exists; otherwise restore old constraints/columns and drop only new tables/triggers/indexes. Do not delete data to force downgrade.

## Selection, projection and capability

One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work.

`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` and `fact_age` derive from recorded gateway diagnostic/contact plus accepted last fact separately; no source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's â€œnext evidenceâ€ is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.

Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

## Money schema `0023` and rules

Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). All UUID references below are composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` FKs, including Site, FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes.

* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)` and `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)`. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `RECORDED_REPAIR` is `UNPRICED`, quantity null/`none`, until an exact accepted cost feed and non-overlap rule exist. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; typed nullable locators for FindingRevision/episode revision/assumption revision/source gate, `window_start,window_end`, `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on that named unique target. A trigger on cold FindingRevision and episode revision enqueues the shared Site/window/room; assumption/source-gate triggers enqueue only overlapping cold scopes. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently.
* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.

`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`. An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.

The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` watches `financial_assumption_revision` for only the three reserved cold keys; the current publisher refuses them, so this arm has no W2 fixture rows and future publication requires its own approval/migration. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.

`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.

`PostgresColdConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

## Five API reads

`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{code}/cold`. Request query for all is `from,to,known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `after,limit` (default 50, max 200). Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. 401 unauthenticated, 403 tenant/source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site, 409 unresolved selected revision or cursor/basis conflict, 422 malformed/range/unknown query, 503 store unavailable use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

| GET suffix | Exact additional response |
| --- | --- |
| `/overview` | `status:{state:OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `objective:{text,policy_low_deg_c,policy_high_deg_c,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `temperature_summary:{latest_deg_c,latest_at,coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio},episode_count,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `backup_summary:{attempts,observed_success,observed_failure,unknown,latest_chain_id,as_of,limitation}`, `causal_strip:[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `findings:{count,items:[{finding_id,revision_id,title,claim_level,link}],as_of,limitation}`, `needs_attention:[{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}]`, `consequence_summary:{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|RECORDED,quantity:{low,high,unit:'degC_min'},amount:{low:null,high:null,currency:null},overlap_group,as_of,limitation}`, `links:{temperature,backup,findings,evidence,analysis,financials}`. The response includes W2-03 selected temperature points only through its linked read, never repackages model truth. |
| `/evidence` | `capabilities:[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage,as_of,limitation}]`, `sources:[{id,role,contact,fact_age,accepted_count,last_accepted_at,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{analysis,foundation,temperature,backup}`. `contact` is `RECENT|STALE|NEVER|UNKNOWN`; `fact_age` is duration/null, never equipment condition. |
| `/evidence/analysis` | `accepted_records:{items:[{evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id,link,as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`, `claim_rules:[{rule_id,requirement,observed_roles,missing_roles,coverage,result,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{evidence,findings}`. No private oracle or rejected ordinal masquerades as accepted. |
| `/financials` | `consequences:[{id,kind,state,quantity_bounds:{low,high},quantity_unit,amount_bounds:{low:null,high:null},currency:null,reason,overlap_group,exact_basis,as_of,limitation}]`, `not_included:[{code:STOCK_VALUE_UNDECLARED|EXPOSURE_RULE_UNAPPROVED|SPOILAGE_UNPROVED|REPAIR_COST_ABSENT|SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD,text,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{assumptions,findings,evidence}`. Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
| `/financial-assumptions` | `items:[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,source,as_of,limitation}]`, `missing_keys:['cold_stock_quantity','cold_stock_unit_value','cold_exposure_rule']` in this slice, `publication_state:'NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_W2'`, `links:{financials}`. `items=[]` for six canonical replays. This is a read-only explicit absence statement, not a publication form. |

Links carry the exact selected `from,to,known_at` and record/revision ID through route builders, URL encoded. The Site overview's Finding count counts distinct current Finding IDs, not episode revisions. `needs_attention` is ordered: pending recompute, probe gap, observed start failure, unknown transfer, missing policy; omit a code only when its predicate is false. No â€œnext actionâ€ link unless an actual W2-03 Finding or Evidence destination exists.

 succeeded in 504ms:
"""Dispatch recompute worker (S3-01 phase 2).

Leases the S2 `dispatch` recompute jobs, expands each job's source-time window
by the continuity bracket to the UTC-day assessment windows it can affect
(the affected window and its continuity neighbours), assesses each window at
the lease's knowledge cutoff through the typed seam, and commits every run,
revision and the job completion together. A failure releases the job for a
retry with back-off; after `max_attempts` the job is FAILED with its reason.

An acceptance that arrives while a job is leased cannot widen that job (S2
coalesces only into PENDING jobs), so it creates a new PENDING job: the next
pass reassesses with the new evidence and nothing is lost.

`reconcile` schedules windows whose last selected default run used another
model version or a configuration that the current selection no longer gives (a
backdated Foundation or binding publication); every `run_pending` pass runs it
for each assessed Site first.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import ModuleType
from typing import Any, Callable
from uuid import UUID

from assetops_backend.analytics import dispatch_v3
from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_inputs import (
    BRACKET,
    AnalyticInputSet,
    CohortMember,
    EvidenceQuery,
    EvidenceQueryResult,
    Window,
    build_input_set,
    sha256_json,
    watermarks,
)
from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_ports import (
    AssessmentStore,
    Configuration,
    JobLease,
    LeaseLost,
    PersistedRun,
    RunOutput,
)
from assetops_backend.analytics.cohort import select_cohort
from assetops_backend.sites.binding_roles import (
    ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_1,
    ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_2,
    ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_3,
    roles_for,
)

DOMAIN = "dispatch"
DAY = timedelta(days=1)
DISPATCH_ROLES = (dispatch_v3.GEN_STATE, dispatch_v3.GEN_POWER, dispatch_v3.LOAD, dispatch_v3.PV_AVAILABLE,
                  dispatch_v3.DISCHARGE, dispatch_v3.SOC)
ROLES_BY_SET = {s: tuple(roles_for(s)) for s in (ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_1, ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_2,
                                                    ROLE_SET_MINIGRID_3)}


def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def day_windows(start: datetime, end: datetime, bracket: timedelta = BRACKET) -> list[Window]:
    """UTC-day windows intersecting `[start - bracket, end + bracket)`."""
    lo = (start - bracket).astimezone(timezone.utc)
    day = lo.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    out = []
    while day < end + bracket:
        out.append(Window(day, day + DAY))
        day += DAY
    return out


def configuration_sha256(config: Configuration) -> str:
    return sha256_json({
        "controls": sorted((c.payload() for c in config.controls), key=lambda p: sha256_json(p)),
        "bindings": sorted([b.role, str(b.asset_id), str(b.valid_from), str(b.valid_to),
                            str(b.binding_revision_id), b.role_set] for b in config.bindings),
        "role_sets": sorted(config.role_sets), "limits": sorted(config.limits),
    })


def input_basis(result: EvidenceQueryResult, model: ModuleType | Any = dispatch_v3) -> tuple[AnalyticInputSet, str]:
    """Cohort selection and the frozen input set of one query result, with its configuration digest.
    The worker assesses it; the phase-3 read compares its `input_sha256` with the selected run."""
    selection = select_cohort(result.readings, result.query.window, result.query.cohort, result.attestations)
    inputs = build_input_set(result, selection, analytic_id=model.ANALYTIC_ID, model_version=model.MODEL_VERSION,
                             roles_by_set=ROLES_BY_SET)
    config = Configuration(result.bindings, result.controls, result.role_sets, result.configuration_limits)
    return inputs, configuration_sha256(config)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JobOutcome:
    job_id: UUID
    status: str  # DONE | RETRY | FAILED | LEASE_LOST
    runs: tuple[PersistedRun, ...] = ()
    error: str | None = None


class DispatchAssessmentWorker:
    def __init__(self, store: AssessmentStore, *, model: ModuleType | Any = dispatch_v3,
                 clock: Callable[[], datetime] = utc_now, lease: timedelta = timedelta(minutes=5),
                 max_attempts: int = 3, backoff: timedelta = timedelta(seconds=30)) -> None:
        self.store, self.model, self.clock = store, model, clock
        self.lease, self.max_attempts, self.backoff = lease, max_attempts, backoff

    # -- one window -------------------------------------------------------------
    def compute(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, window: Window, knowledge_at: datetime,
                cohort: tuple[CohortMember, ...] | None = None) -> RunOutput:
        started = self.clock()
        query = EvidenceQuery(tenant_id, site_id, DOMAIN, window, knowledge_at, cohort=cohort, roles=DISPATCH_ROLES)
        inputs, config_sha = input_basis(self.store.query_evidence(query), self.model)
        assessment = self.model.assess(inputs)
        return RunOutput(inputs, assessment, config_sha, watermarks(inputs.readings),
                         "DEFAULT" if cohort is None else "EXPLICIT", started)

    def assess_explicit(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, window: Window,
                        cohort: tuple[CohortMember, ...], knowledge_at: datetime | None = None) -> PersistedRun:
        """An operator's explicit cohort choice for one window (persisted under a cohort-suffixed key)."""
        out = self.compute(tenant_id, site_id, window, knowledge_at or self.clock(), cohort)
        return self.store.record_runs([out], self.clock())[0]

    # -- jobs -------------------------------------------------------------------
    def run_once(self, tenant_id: UUID | None = None, site_id: UUID | None = None) -> JobOutcome | None:
        """Lease and process one due job: the global oldest, or with `tenant_id`/`site_id` that Site's oldest."""
        scope = {} if site_id is None else {"tenant_id": tenant_id, "site_id": site_id}
        lease = self.store.lease_job(DOMAIN, self.clock(), self.lease, max_attempts=self.max_attempts, **scope)
        if lease is None:
            return None
        try:
            computed = [self.compute(lease.tenant_id, lease.site_id, w, lease.knowledge_cutoff)
                        for w in day_windows(lease.window.start, lease.window.end)]
            # A neighbour with no accepted reading in it has nothing to assess (evidence is
            # append-only, so it never had a run either); it stays UNASSESSED.
            outputs = [o for o in computed if o.inputs.cohort.status != "EMPTY"]
            runs = self.store.complete_job(lease, outputs, self.clock())
            return JobOutcome(lease.job_id, "DONE", tuple(runs))
        except LeaseLost as lost:
            return JobOutcome(lease.job_id, "LEASE_LOST", error=str(lost))
        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - every failure is recorded on the job and retried
            reason = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:500]
            status = self.store.fail_job(lease, reason, self.clock(), max_attempts=self.max_attempts,
                                         backoff=self.backoff)
            return JobOutcome(lease.job_id, status, error=reason)

    def run_pending(self, limit: int = 1000, *, reconcile: bool = True) -> list[JobOutcome]:
        """Each pass first reconciles every assessed Site, so a backdated Foundation/binding
        publication or a model-version change is enqueued without an operator (phase-2 F4)."""
        if reconcile:
            self.reconcile_all()
        outcomes = []
        for _ in range(limit):
            outcome = self.run_once()
            if outcome is None:
                break
            outcomes.append(outcome)
        return outcomes

    def run_site(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, limit: int) -> list[JobOutcome]:
        """One Site's bounded S3 stage (S4-02d): reconcile this Site (a never-assessed Site has no heads and
        enqueues nothing), then process at most `limit` of its due jobs, leased only from this Site."""
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("A Site stage processes at least one job.")
        self.reconcile(tenant_id, site_id)
        outcomes = []
        for _ in range(limit):
            outcome = self.run_once(tenant_id, site_id)
            if outcome is None:
                break
            outcomes.append(outcome)
        return outcomes

    def reconcile_all(self) -> list[Window]:
        scheduled: list[Window] = []
        for tenant_id, site_id in self.store.assessed_sites(self.model.ANALYTIC_ID):
            scheduled += self.reconcile(tenant_id, site_id)
        return scheduled

    def reconcile(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> list[Window]:
        """Enqueue windows whose latest default run used another model version or a configuration
        the current selection no longer gives (backdated Foundation/binding publication)."""
        now = self.clock()
        scheduled = []
        for head in self.store.run_heads(tenant_id, site_id, self.model.ANALYTIC_ID):
            stale = head.model_version != self.model.MODEL_VERSION
            if not stale:
                config = self.store.configuration(tenant_id, site_id, head.window.widened(BRACKET), now)
                stale = configuration_sha256(config) != head.configuration_sha256
            if stale:
                self.store.enqueue(tenant_id, site_id, DOMAIN, head.window, now)
                scheduled.append(head.window)
        return scheduled

                  site_id: UUID | None = None) -> JobLease | None:
        """Lease the oldest due job of `domain`; with `tenant_id` and `site_id`, only that Site's jobs (S4-02d:
        the schedule leases per Site, which the global FIFO cannot make fair). Both the expire-to-FAILED step and
        the lease are scoped the same way."""
        if (tenant_id is None) != (site_id is None):
            raise ValueError("A Site-scoped lease names both the tenant and the Site.")
        scope = "" if site_id is None else " AND tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s"
        scoped = {} if site_id is None else {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}

        def work(c: Connection) -> JobLease | None:
            # A lease that expired on its last allowed attempt (the worker crashed or always outlives
            # its lease) is FAILED with a reason instead of being re-leased forever (phase-2 F6).
            c.execute(text(
                "UPDATE recompute_job SET status = 'FAILED', lease_until = NULL, last_error = "
                "'lease expired on attempt ' || attempts || ' of ' || CAST(:max AS integer) || "
                "' without completing' WHERE domain = :d AND status = 'LEASED' AND lease_until < :now "
                "AND attempts >= :max" + scope), {"d": domain, "now": now, "max": max_attempts, **scoped})
            row = c.execute(text(
                "UPDATE recompute_job SET status = 'LEASED', lease_until = :until, attempts = attempts + 1 "
                "WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM recompute_job WHERE domain = :d AND ((status = 'PENDING' AND "
                "available_at <= :now) OR (status = 'LEASED' AND lease_until < :now))" + scope +
                " ORDER BY available_at, created_at LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED) "
                "RETURNING id, tenant_id, site_id, domain, window_start, window_end, attempts"),
                {"d": domain, "now": now, "until": now + lease, **scoped}).first()
            if row is None:
                return None
            return JobLease(row[0], row[1], row[2], row[3], Window(row[4], row[5]), row[6], now)

        return self._run(work, write=True)

    def _still_mine(self, c: Connection, lease: JobLease) -> None:
        row = c.execute(text("SELECT status, attempts FROM recompute_job WHERE id = :id FOR UPDATE"),
                        {"id": lease.job_id}).first()
        if row is None or row[0] != "LEASED" or row[1] != lease.attempts:
            raise LeaseLost(f"job {lease.job_id} is {row[0] if row else 'missing'} (attempt "
                            f"{row[1] if row else '-'}), not our attempt {lease.attempts}")

    def complete_job(self, lease: JobLease, outputs: list[RunOutput], now: datetime) -> list[PersistedRun]:
        def work(c: Connection) -> list[PersistedRun]:
            self._still_mine(c, lease)
            runs = [self._persist(c, out, lease.job_id, now) for out in outputs]
            c.execute(text("UPDATE recompute_job SET status = 'DONE', lease_until = NULL, last_error = NULL "
                           "WHERE id = :id"), {"id": lease.job_id})
            return runs

        return self._run(work, write=True)

    def fail_job(self, lease: JobLease, error: str, now: datetime, *, max_attempts: int,
                 backoff: timedelta) -> str:
        def work(c: Connection) -> str:
            self._still_mine(c, lease)
            final = lease.attempts >= max_attempts
            c.execute(text(
                "UPDATE recompute_job SET status = :st, lease_until = NULL, available_at = :at, last_error = :e "
                "WHERE id = :id"),
                # A FAILED job keeps its failure instant in available_at (never reused): a later DEFAULT
                # selection covering its window supersedes it (_LIVE_JOBS).
                {"st": "FAILED" if final else "PENDING", "at": now if final else now + backoff * lease.attempts,
                 "e": error,
                 "id": lease.job_id})
            return "FAILED" if final else "RETRY"

        try:
            return self._run(work, write=True)
        except LeaseLost:
            return "LEASE_LOST"

    def record_runs(self, outputs: list[RunOutput], now: datetime) -> list[PersistedRun]:
        return self._run(lambda c: [self._persist(c, out, None, now) for out in outputs], write=True)

    def enqueue(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, domain: str, window: Window, now: datetime) -> None:
        self._run(lambda c: self._enqueue(c, tenant_id, site_id, domain, window, now), write=True)

    @staticmethod
    def _enqueue(c: Connection, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, domain: str, window: Window,
                 now: datetime) -> None:
        c.execute(text(_JOB_LOCK), {"site": site_id, "d": domain})
        job = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT id, window_start, window_end FROM recompute_job WHERE site_id = :site AND domain = :d "
            "AND status = 'PENDING' AND window_start <= :end AND window_end >= :start "
            "ORDER BY window_start LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE"),
            {"site": site_id, "d": domain, "start": window.start, "end": window.end}).first()
        if job is None:
            c.execute(text(
                "INSERT INTO recompute_job (id, tenant_id, site_id, domain, window_start, window_end, "
                "cause_evidence_id, status, available_at, created_at) "
                "VALUES (:id, :t, :site, :d, :start, :end, NULL, 'PENDING', :now, :now)"),
                {"id": uuid4(), "t": tenant_id, "site": site_id, "d": domain, "start": window.start,
                 "end": window.end, "now": now})
            return
        c.execute(text("UPDATE recompute_job SET window_start = :start, window_end = :end WHERE id = :id"),
                  {"start": min(window.start, job[1]), "end": max(window.end, job[2]), "id": job[0]})

    # --- clock attestations (LR-01) ------------------------------------------------

    def site_scope(self, tenant_slug: str, site_code: str) -> tuple[str, UUID, UUID] | None:
        """(site_code, tenant_id, site_id) of an imported Site inside the named tenant, else None."""
        return self._run(lambda c: (lambda row: None if row is None else (row[0], row[1], row[2]))(c.execute(text(
            "SELECT s.site_code, s.tenant_id, s.id FROM site s JOIN tenant t ON t.id = s.tenant_id "
            "WHERE t.slug = :slug AND s.site_code_key = lower(:code)"), {"slug": tenant_slug, "code": site_code}
        ).first()))

    def clock_attestation_events(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> list[ClockAttestation]:
        return self._run(lambda c: [_attestation(r) for r in c.execute(text(
            f"SELECT {_ATTESTATION_COLUMNS} FROM source_clock_attestation WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
            "ORDER BY recorded_at, attestation_id, version"), {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).all()])

    def record_clock_attestation(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, change: AttestationChange, principal,
                                 now: datetime, *, domain: str = "dispatch",
                                 bracket: timedelta = BRACKET) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Append one attestation event for the SYSTEM attester and, in the same transaction, requeue every
        accepted reading of the stream whose measurement interval touches the old or the new validity (creation,
        replacement, expiry and revocation alike). A retry with the same idempotency key and request answers the
        stored event and queues nothing; the same key with another request is a conflict."""
        validate_change(change, principal)
        request_sha = sha256_json({k: (None if v is None else str(v)) for k, v in asdict(change).items()})

        def work(c: Connection) -> dict[str, Any]:
            c.execute(text(_ASSESS_LOCK), {"site": site_id, "suffix": ":clock-attestation"})
            held = c.execute(text(
                f"SELECT {_ATTESTATION_COLUMNS}, request_sha256 FROM source_clock_attestation WHERE tenant_id = :t "
                "AND site_id = :s AND idempotency_key = :key"),
                {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "key": change.idempotency_key}).first()
            if held is not None:
                if held[13] != request_sha:
                    raise AttestationRefused(409, "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", "This idempotency key already recorded "
                                             "another attestation request.")
                return {"event": _attestation(held), "created": False, "requeued": []}
            site = c.execute(text("SELECT proposed_source_mode FROM site WHERE tenant_id = :t AND id = :s"),
                             {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).first()
            if site is None:
                raise AttestationRefused(404, "SITE_NOT_FOUND", "No such Site in this tenant.")
            if site[0] != "SIMULATED":
                raise AttestationRefused(403, "SIMULATED_PROOF_FOR_LIVE_SITE", "A simulated attestation cannot "
                                         "prove a real source's clock; this Site is not simulated.")
            if c.execute(text("SELECT 1 FROM source WHERE id = :src AND tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s"),
                         {"src": change.source_id, "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).first() is None:
                raise AttestationRefused(404, "SOURCE_NOT_IN_SITE", "The source is not registered on this Site.")
            prior = None
            if change.attestation_id is not None:
                row = c.execute(text(
                    f"SELECT {_ATTESTATION_COLUMNS} FROM source_clock_attestation WHERE attestation_id = :a "
                    "AND tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s ORDER BY version DESC LIMIT 1"),
                    {"a": change.attestation_id, "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).first()
                prior = None if row is None else _attestation(row)
            event = next_event(change, prior, event_id=uuid4(), attestation_id=uuid4(), recorded_at=now)
            c.execute(text(
                "INSERT INTO source_clock_attestation (id, attestation_id, version, tenant_id, site_id, source_id, "
                "sequence_epoch, event_kind, proof_class, authority, label, max_utc_error_ms, valid_from, valid_to, "
                "reason, recorded_at, recorded_by, authorization_basis, channel, idempotency_key, request_sha256) "
                "VALUES (:id, :a, :v, :t, :s, :src, :ep, :kind, :proof, :auth, :label, :err, :vf, :vt, :reason, "
                ":now, :by, :basis, :channel, :key, :sha)"),
                {"id": event.event_id, "a": event.attestation_id, "v": event.version, "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id,
                 "src": event.source_id, "ep": event.sequence_epoch, "kind": event.event_kind,
                 "proof": event.proof_class, "auth": event.authority, "label": event.label,
                 "err": int(event.max_utc_error / timedelta(milliseconds=1)), "vf": event.valid_from,
                 "vt": event.valid_to, "reason": change.reason, "now": now, "by": principal.actor_id,
                 "basis": principal.authorization_basis, "channel": change.channel, "key": change.idempotency_key,
                 "sha": request_sha})
            intervals: set[tuple[datetime, datetime]] = set()
            for lo, hi in {(a.valid_from, a.valid_to) for a in (prior, event) if a is not None}:
                intervals.update((r[0], r[1]) for r in c.execute(text(_STREAM_READINGS), {
                    "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "src": event.source_id, "ep": event.sequence_epoch,
                    "lo": lo, "hi": hi}).all())
            spans = requeue_spans(sorted(intervals), bracket)
            for span in spans:
                self._enqueue(c, tenant_id, site_id, domain, span, now)
            return {"event": event, "created": True, "requeued": spans}

        return self._run(work, write=True)

    # --- runs and revisions -------------------------------------------------------

    def _persist(self, c: Connection, out: RunOutput, job_id: UUID | None, now: datetime) -> PersistedRun:
        i = out.inputs
        c.execute(text(_ASSESS_LOCK), {"site": i.site_id, "suffix": ":dispatch-assessment"})
        existing = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT id FROM analytic_run WHERE site_id = :s AND analytic_id = :a AND model_version = :m "
            "AND window_start = :ws AND window_end = :we AND input_sha256 = :h"),
            {"s": i.site_id, "a": i.analytic_id, "m": i.model_version, "ws": i.window.start, "we": i.window.end,
             "h": i.input_sha256}).first()
        tenant = c.execute(text("SELECT tenant_id FROM site WHERE id = :s"), {"s": i.site_id}).scalar_one()
        if existing is not None:
            linked = c.execute(text("SELECT assessment_revision_id FROM analytic_run_assessment WHERE run_id = :r"),
                               {"r": existing[0]}).scalars().all()
            self._select(c, out, tenant, existing[0], job_id, now)
            return PersistedRun(existing[0], False, tuple(linked), ())
        run_id = uuid4()
        c.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO analytic_run (id, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, model_version, window_start, window_end, "
            "knowledge_cutoff, input_sha256, cohort_status, cohort_sha256, cohort, configuration_sha256, "
            "foundation_revision_ids, binding_set_revision_ids, role_coverage, watermarks, selection, job_id, "
            "status, started_at, completed_at) VALUES (:id, :t, :s, :a, :m, :ws, :we, :k, :h, :cs, :ch, "
            "CAST(:cohort AS jsonb), :cfg, CAST(:fr AS jsonb), CAST(:br AS jsonb), CAST(:cov AS jsonb), "
            "CAST(:wm AS jsonb), :sel, :job, 'COMPLETED', :started, :now)"),
            {"id": run_id, "t": tenant, "s": i.site_id, "a": i.analytic_id, "m": i.model_version,
             "ws": i.window.start, "we": i.window.end, "k": i.knowledge_at, "h": i.input_sha256,
             "cs": i.cohort.status, "ch": i.cohort.identity(), "cohort": json.dumps(i.cohort.payload()),
             "cfg": out.configuration_sha256, "fr": json.dumps(list(i.foundation_revision_ids)),
             "br": json.dumps(list(i.binding_set_revision_ids)), "cov": json.dumps(i.role_coverage),
             "wm": json.dumps(out.watermarks), "sel": out.selection, "job": job_id, "started": out.started_at,
             "now": now})
        if i.readings:
            c.execute(text("INSERT INTO analytic_run_evidence (run_id, evidence_id, tenant_id, site_id) "
                           "VALUES (:r, :e, :t, :s)"),
                      [{"r": run_id, "e": r.evidence_id, "t": tenant, "s": i.site_id} for r in i.readings])
        revision_ids, new_ids = [], []
        a = out.assessment
        items = [("WINDOW", a.assessment_key, a.claim_level, a.result, (), a.basis_sha256)]
        items += [("INTERVAL", x.assessment_key, x.claim_level, x.result, x.evidence_ids, x.basis_sha256)
                  for x in a.intervals]
        for scope, key, claim, result, evidence, basis in items:
            latest = c.execute(text(
                "SELECT id, revision_no, basis_sha256 FROM assessment_revision WHERE site_id = :s "
                "AND analytic_id = :a AND assessment_key = :key ORDER BY revision_no DESC LIMIT 1"),
                {"s": i.site_id, "a": i.analytic_id, "key": key}).first()
            if latest is not None and latest[2] == basis:
                rev_id = latest[0]
            else:
                rev_id = uuid4()
                c.execute(text(
                    "INSERT INTO assessment_revision (id, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, model_version, "
                    "assessment_key, revision_no, analytic_run_id, window_start, window_end, claim_level, result, "
                    "evidence_ids, basis_sha256, created_at) VALUES (:id, :t, :s, :a, :m, :key, :no, :run, :ws, "
                    ":we, :claim, CAST(:result AS jsonb), CAST(:ev AS jsonb), :basis, :now)"),
                    {"id": rev_id, "t": tenant, "s": i.site_id, "a": i.analytic_id, "m": i.model_version, "key": key,
                     "no": 1 if latest is None else latest[1] + 1, "run": run_id, "ws": i.window.start,
                     "we": i.window.end, "claim": claim, "result": json.dumps(result),
                     "ev": json.dumps(list(evidence)), "basis": basis, "now": now})
                new_ids.append(rev_id)
            c.execute(text("INSERT INTO analytic_run_assessment (run_id, assessment_revision_id, tenant_id, site_id, "
                           "scope) VALUES (:r, :rev, :t, :s, :scope)"),
                      {"r": run_id, "rev": rev_id, "t": tenant, "s": i.site_id, "scope": scope})
            revision_ids.append(rev_id)
        self._select(c, out, tenant, run_id, job_id, now)
        return PersistedRun(run_id, True, tuple(revision_ids), tuple(new_ids))

    @staticmethod
    def _select(c: Connection, out: RunOutput, tenant: UUID, run_id: UUID, job_id: UUID | None,
                now: datetime) -> None:
        """Log a DEFAULT selection (created or reused run) in commit order (migration 0008)."""
        if out.selection != "DEFAULT":
            return
        i = out.inputs
        c.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO analytic_run_selection (tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, window_start, window_end, run_id, "
            "job_id, selected_at) VALUES (:t, :s, :a, :ws, :we, :r, :job, :now)"),
            {"t": tenant, "s": i.site_id, "a": i.analytic_id, "ws": i.window.start, "we": i.window.end,
             "r": run_id, "job": job_id, "now": now})

    def run_heads(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str) -> list[RunHead]:
        def work(c: Connection) -> list[RunHead]:
            # The head is the last selection in commit order, not the newest run (a reverted input
            # reuses an older run; migration 0008).
            rows = c.execute(text(
                "SELECT DISTINCT ON (x.window_start, x.window_end) x.window_start, x.window_end, r.model_version, "
                "r.configuration_sha256 FROM analytic_run_selection x JOIN analytic_run r ON r.id = x.run_id "
                "WHERE x.tenant_id = :t AND x.site_id = :s AND x.analytic_id = :a "
                "ORDER BY x.window_start, x.window_end, x.seq DESC"),
                {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "a": analytic_id}).all()
            return [RunHead(Window(r[0], r[1]), r[2], r[3]) for r in rows]

        return self._run(work)

    def assessed_sites(self, analytic_id: str) -> list[tuple[UUID, UUID]]:
        return self._run(lambda c: [(r[0], r[1]) for r in c.execute(text(
            "SELECT DISTINCT tenant_id, site_id FROM analytic_run_selection WHERE analytic_id = :a "
            "ORDER BY tenant_id, site_id"), {"a": analytic_id}).all()])

    def live_jobs(self, analytic_id: str, domain: str, tenant_id: UUID | None = None,
                  site_id: UUID | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Live jobs (unfinished, or FAILED and not yet superseded by a later DEFAULT selection over their window;
        `_LIVE_JOBS`) of every Site, or of one Site, with status, window, attempts, lease and last error (S4-02d
        Site inventory and backlog)."""
        scope = "" if site_id is None else " AND tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s"
        return self._run(lambda c: [dict(r._mapping) for r in c.execute(text(
            "SELECT id, tenant_id, site_id, status, window_start, window_end, attempts, available_at, lease_until, "
            "last_error, created_at FROM recompute_job WHERE domain = :d AND " + _LIVE_JOBS + scope +
            " ORDER BY tenant_id, site_id, available_at, created_at"),
            {"a": analytic_id, "d": domain, "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id})])

    def job(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, job_id: UUID, analytic_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """One job of this tenant/Site (never another Site's), with whether it is still live (`_LIVE_JOBS`)."""
        row = self._run(lambda c: c.execute(text(
            "SELECT id, tenant_id, site_id, domain, status, window_start, window_end, attempts, available_at, "
            "last_error, " + _LIVE_JOBS + " AS live FROM recompute_job WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
            "AND id = :j"), {"a": analytic_id, "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "j": job_id}).first())
        return None if row is None else dict(row._mapping)

    # --- phase-3 reads: selected window, pinned run, revision drill-down -----------

    def _snapshot(self, work):
        """One REPEATABLE READ transaction: every part of a read sees one committed state."""
        engine = self._connect_engine()
        try:
            with engine.connect().execution_options(isolation_level="REPEATABLE READ") as connection:
                with connection.begin():
                    return work(connection)
        except (OperationalError, ProgrammingError) as error:
            raise AssessmentStoreUnavailable(f"The assessment store failed: {error.orig}") from error

    def window_snapshot(self, query: EvidenceQuery, analytic_id: str,
                        basis: Callable[[EvidenceQueryResult], tuple[str | None, str]]) -> WindowSnapshot:
        """The current accepted basis, the last DEFAULT selection, its revisions and every overlapping
        unfinished or failed job of one window, read in one snapshot (Architect Q7)."""
        return self._snapshot(lambda c: self._window(c, query, analytic_id, basis))

    def claim_limit_snapshot(self, query: EvidenceQuery, analytic_id: str,
                             basis: Callable[[EvidenceQueryResult], tuple[str | None, str]],
                             revision_id: UUID | None) -> tuple[WindowSnapshot, dict[str, Any] | None]:
        """S3-02a: the window snapshot and one exact revision's detail in the same REPEATABLE READ snapshot,
        so currentness and the pinned revision can never come from two committed states."""
        def work(c: Connection) -> tuple[WindowSnapshot, dict[str, Any] | None]:
            snap = self._window(c, query, analytic_id, basis)
            detail = None if revision_id is None else self._revision_detail(
                c, query.tenant_id, query.site_id, revision_id)
            return snap, detail

        return self._snapshot(work)

    def _window(self, c: Connection, query: EvidenceQuery, analytic_id: str,
                basis: Callable[[EvidenceQueryResult], tuple[str | None, str]]) -> WindowSnapshot:
        w = query.window
        rng = query.read_range
        rows = c.execute(text(_EVIDENCE), {
            "t": query.tenant_id, "s": query.site_id, "k": query.knowledge_at, "lo": rng.start, "hi": rng.end,
            "any_role": not query.roles, "roles": list(query.roles)}).all()
        readings = tuple(_reading(r) for r in rows)
        config = self._configuration(c, query.tenant_id, query.site_id, rng, query.knowledge_at)
        input_sha, config_sha = basis(EvidenceQueryResult(
            query, readings, config.bindings, config.controls, config.role_sets, config.limits,
            _attestations(c, query.tenant_id, query.site_id, query.knowledge_at)))
        p = {"t": query.tenant_id, "s": query.site_id, "a": analytic_id, "ws": w.start, "we": w.end,
             "d": query.domain, "lo": rng.start, "hi": rng.end}
        head = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT seq, selected_at, job_id, run_id FROM analytic_run_selection WHERE tenant_id = :t "
            "AND site_id = :s AND analytic_id = :a AND window_start = :ws AND window_end = :we "
            "ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1"), p).first()
        jobs = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT id, status, window_start, window_end, attempts, last_error, created_at, available_at "
            "FROM recompute_job WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s AND domain = :d "
            f"AND {_LIVE_JOBS} AND window_start < :hi AND window_end >= :lo "
            "ORDER BY created_at, id"), p).all()
        run = None if head is None else self._run_detail(c, query.tenant_id, query.site_id, head[3])
        keys = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT DISTINCT ON (assessment_key) assessment_key, id, revision_no, claim_level, created_at "
            "FROM assessment_revision WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s AND analytic_id = :a "
            "AND window_start = :ws AND window_end = :we ORDER BY assessment_key, revision_no DESC"), p).all()
        read_at = c.execute(text("SELECT clock_timestamp()")).scalar_one()
        return WindowSnapshot(
            reading_count=len(readings), current_input_sha256=input_sha, current_configuration_sha256=config_sha,
            head=None if head is None else {"seq": head[0], "selected_at": head[1], "job_id": head[2]},
            run=run,
            jobs=[dict(zip(("id", "status", "window_start", "window_end", "attempts", "last_error",
                            "created_at", "available_at"), j)) for j in jobs],_EVIDENCE_NAMES = ("evidence_id", "envelope_sha256", "message_id", "batch_id", "external_batch_id", "gateway_id",
                   "source_id", "sequence_epoch", "sequence", "device_id", "signal_id", "role", "asset_id",
                   "source_time", "interval_start", "interval_end", "published_at", "received_at", "accepted_at",
                   "quality", "unavailable_reason", "value", "unit", "source_mode", "foundation_revision_id",
                   "binding_set_revision_id", "binding_revision_id", "disposition")


def _reading(r) -> AcceptedReading:
    return AcceptedReading(
        evidence_id=r[0], envelope_sha256=r[1], batch_id=r[2], gateway_id=r[3], source_id=r[4],
        sequence_epoch=r[5], sequence=r[6], role=r[7], asset_id=r[8], source_time=r[9],
        interval_start=r[10], interval_end=r[11], published_at=r[12], received_at=r[13], accepted_at=r[14],
        quality=r[15], value=r[16], unit=r[17], foundation_revision_id=r[18],
        binding_set_revision_id=r[19], binding_revision_id=r[20], source_mode=r[21])


def _revision_row(r) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return dict(zip(_REVISION_NAMES, r))


def _evidence(c: Connection, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, ids) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Exact accepted Evidence by stored ID (never re-selected by time or `knowledge_at`)."""
    if not ids:
        return []
    rows = c.execute(text(
        "SELECT e.id, e.envelope_sha256, e.message_id, e.batch_id, b.external_batch_id, e.gateway_id, "
        "e.source_id, e.sequence_epoch, e.sequence, e.device_id, e.signal_id, e.role, e.asset_id, "
        "e.source_time, e.interval_start, e.interval_end, e.published_at, e.received_at, d.decided_at, "
        "e.quality, e.unavailable_reason, e.normalized_value_text, e.normalized_unit, e.source_mode, "
        "e.foundation_revision_id, e.binding_set_revision_id, e.binding_revision_id, d.decision "
        "FROM accepted_evidence e JOIN ingestion_batch b ON b.id = e.batch_id "
        "JOIN ingestion_disposition d ON d.batch_id = e.batch_id AND d.ordinal = e.ordinal "
        "WHERE e.tenant_id = :t AND e.site_id = :s AND e.id = ANY(:ids) ORDER BY e.source_time, e.id"),
        {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "ids": [UUID(str(i)) for i in ids]}).all()
    return [dict(zip(_EVIDENCE_NAMES, r)) for r in rows]
