"""Pure cold-chain assessment rules: `cold-excursion/1` and `cold-start-response/1` (W2-03).

Authority: `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`, `.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md` ("Inputs, model and selection",
"Rules and exact outcomes") and `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` section 5.

The rules read only accepted public evidence that the caller has already selected for one Site, one fixed
half-open window `[from_at, to_at)` and one knowledge cutoff `known_at`, together with the effective
configuration (room policy, return hysteresis, minimum excursion, start timeout, the bound probe/room/generator
assets) and the exact S4-05 source gate. A reading qualifies only when it is bound to the effective
`cold-roles-1` role and asset, GOOD and clock-attested; SUSPECT readings stay linked as CONTEXT, and unbound,
clock-unattested and contradictory records stay linked as EXCLUDED with their reason. Nothing here persists,
reads a clock, or consults private simulation state.

Claims stay bounded: an excursion is bounded by observed samples only (no interpolation across a gap of more
than ten minutes, no continuous curve), degree minutes are sums of observed rectangles, the peak is the maximum
observed sample. No spoilage, product-loss or failed-part claim is made.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from uuid import UUID

from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_inputs import iso
from assetops_contracts.source_envelope import canonical_json

EXCURSION_ANALYTIC = "cold-excursion"
START_RESPONSE_ANALYTIC = "cold-start-response"
MODEL_VERSION = "1"
DOMAIN = "cold_chain"

CLAIM_LEVELS = ("OBSERVED", "BOUNDED", "POSSIBLE", "UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY")
EXCURSION_STATES = ("OBSERVED_EXCURSION", "NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED", "UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY")
START_RESULTS = ("OBSERVED_SUCCESS", "OBSERVED_FAILURE", "UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY")
STEPS = ("GRID", "START_REQUEST", "START_RESULT", "GENERATOR", "TRANSFER", "BUS", "COMPRESSOR", "PROBE")
GAP_REASONS = ("MISSING_PROBE", "SUSPECT", "CLOCK_UNATTESTED", "CONTRADICTORY", "UNBOUND")
USES = ("SUPPORT", "CONTEXT", "EXCLUDED")

SLOT = timedelta(minutes=5)
MAX_BRIDGE = timedelta(minutes=10)
ORDER_TOLERANCE = timedelta(seconds=30)
COVERAGE_NEEDED = Decimal("0.8")

PROBE_ROLE = "chamber_temperature"
START_ROLE = "backup_start_event"
GRID_EVENT_ROLE = "grid_power_event"
GENERATOR_STATE_ROLE = "generator_state"
TRANSFER_ROLE = "transfer_result"
BUS_ROLE = "backup_bus_state"
COMPRESSOR_ROLE = "compressor_state"
DOOR_ROLE = "door_event"
#: Event kinds each event role carries, so an unbound event still links as EXCLUDED to the chain it names.
_ROLE_EVENT_KINDS = {
    START_ROLE: ("START_REQUEST", "START_RESULT"),
    GRID_EVENT_ROLE: ("POWER_LOSS", "POWER_RESTORED"),
    TRANSFER_ROLE: ("TRANSFER_RESULT",),
}
_EPOCH = datetime(1970, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdEvidence:
    """One accepted public record as the selection read it. `role`/`asset_id` are the effective
    `cold-roles-1` binding at the record's source time (None when unbound); `value` is a Decimal for numeric
    readings, the state text for state readings, None for events."""

    evidence_id: UUID
    envelope_sha256: str
    family: str
    record_kind: str | None
    role: str | None
    asset_id: UUID | None
    source_time: datetime
    received_at: datetime
    decided_at: datetime
    quality: str
    value: Decimal | str | None
    unit: str | None
    event_id: str | None
    correlation_id: str | None
    outcome: str | None
    source_id: str
    clock_attested: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdWindow:
    """UTC half-open `[from_at, to_at)` and the knowledge cutoff on server receipt/decision time."""

    from_at: datetime
    to_at: datetime
    known_at: datetime

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.from_at < self.to_at:
            raise ValueError("a cold window needs from_at < to_at")

    def expected_instants(self) -> tuple[datetime, ...]:
        """Five-minute UTC-aligned instants `t` with `from_at <= t < to_at`."""
        offset = (self.from_at - _EPOCH) % SLOT
        first = self.from_at if offset == timedelta(0) else self.from_at + (SLOT - offset)
        out = []
        t = first
        while t < self.to_at:
            out.append(t)
            t += SLOT
        return tuple(out)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdPolicy:
    """Effective room configuration for `cold-excursion/1`."""

    room_asset_id: UUID
    probe_asset_id: UUID
    policy_low: Decimal
    policy_high: Decimal
    return_hysteresis: Decimal
    min_excursion_minutes: Decimal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdStartPolicy:
    """Effective generator configuration for `cold-start-response/1`; `transfer_bound` says the optional
    `transfer_result` role is bound, so a missing transfer is a missing step."""

    generator_asset_id: UUID
    start_timeout_minutes: Decimal
    transfer_bound: bool
    probe_asset_id: UUID | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceGate:
    """The exact S4-05 source classification and source IDs, and whether it permits a Finding."""

    classification: str
    source_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    permitted: bool

    def basis(self) -> dict:
        return {"classification": self.classification, "source_ids": sorted(self.source_ids)}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdLink:
    evidence_id: UUID
    use: str
    reason: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdGap:
    start: datetime
    end: datetime
    reason: str
    expected_points: int
    received_points: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdExcursion:
    direction: str  # HIGH | LOW
    anchor_evidence_id: UUID
    anchor_time: datetime
    start_earliest: datetime | None
    start_latest: datetime
    end_earliest: datetime | None
    end_latest: datetime | None
    open: bool
    duration_low: Decimal
    duration_high: Decimal | None
    degree_minutes_low: Decimal
    degree_minutes_high: Decimal
    peak_observed: Decimal | None
    claim_level: str
    exposure_finding: bool
    links: tuple[ColdLink, ...]
    limitation: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdStep:
    step: str
    state: str  # OBSERVED | MISSING | CONTRADICTORY
    at: datetime | None
    evidence_ids: tuple[UUID, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdStartChain:
    correlation_id: str
    generator_asset_id: UUID
    anchor_evidence_id: UUID
    anchor_time: datetime
    steps: tuple[ColdStep, ...]
    missing_steps: tuple[str, ...]
    result: str
    claim_level: str
    start_timeout_min: Decimal
    failure_finding: bool
    links: tuple[ColdLink, ...]
    limitation: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ColdAssessment:
    analytic_id: str
    model_version: str
    window: ColdWindow
    state: str
    claim_level: str
    known_points: int
    expected_points: int
    coverage: Decimal
    gaps: tuple[ColdGap, ...] = ()
    excursions: tuple[ColdExcursion, ...] = ()
    chains: tuple[ColdStartChain, ...] = ()
    links: tuple[ColdLink, ...] = ()
    limitations: tuple[str, ...] = field(default=())


# --- identity ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def episode_key_sha256(tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, kind: str, subject_asset_id: UUID,
                       anchor_evidence_id: UUID) -> str:
    """SHA-256 of canonical JSON `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,anchor_evidence_id]`."""
    document = [str(tenant_id).lower(), str(site_id).lower(), kind, str(subject_asset_id).lower(),
                str(anchor_evidence_id).lower()]
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(document).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def basis_sha256(*, analytic_id: str, model_version: str, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, window: ColdWindow,
                 foundation_revision_ids: Iterable[UUID], binding_revision_ids: Iterable[UUID],
                 source_gate: SourceGate, records: Iterable[ColdEvidence]) -> str:
    """SHA-256 of the canonical basis document; records sorted by `(evidence_id, envelope_sha256)`."""
    rows = sorted({(str(r.evidence_id).lower(), r.envelope_sha256) for r in records})
    document = {
        "analytic_id": analytic_id,
        "model_version": model_version,
        "tenant_id": str(tenant_id).lower(),
        "site_id": str(site_id).lower(),
        "window": {"from": iso(window.from_at), "to": iso(window.to_at)},
        "known_at": iso(window.known_at),
        "foundation_revision_ids": sorted({str(i).lower() for i in foundation_revision_ids}),
        "binding_revision_ids": sorted({str(i).lower() for i in binding_revision_ids}),
        "source_gate": source_gate.basis(),
        "records": [{"evidence_id": e, "envelope_sha256": s} for e, s in rows],
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(document).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


# --- selection helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _known(evidence: Iterable[ColdEvidence], window: ColdWindow) -> list[ColdEvidence]:
    rows = [e for e in evidence if window.from_at <= e.source_time < window.to_at
            and e.received_at <= window.known_at and e.decided_at <= window.known_at]
    return sorted(rows, key=lambda e: (e.source_time, e.received_at, str(e.evidence_id)))


def _exclusion(e: ColdEvidence, role: str, asset_id: UUID | None) -> str | None:
    """Why a record of `role` cannot support a claim, or None when it qualifies."""
    if e.role is None or (asset_id is not None and e.asset_id != asset_id):
        return "UNBOUND"
    if not e.clock_attested:
        return "CLOCK_UNATTESTED"
    if e.quality == "SUSPECT":
        return "SUSPECT"
    if e.quality != "GOOD":
        return e.quality
    return None


def _use(reason: str) -> str:
    return "CONTEXT" if reason == "SUSPECT" else "EXCLUDED"


def _minutes(delta: timedelta) -> Decimal:
    return Decimal(int(delta.total_seconds())) / Decimal(60)


def _is_probe_candidate(e: ColdEvidence, policy: ColdPolicy) -> bool:
    if e.family != "MEASUREMENT":
        return False
    if e.role == PROBE_ROLE:
        return True
    # An unbound temperature reading is still a probe-shaped record the gap must name.
    return e.role is None and e.unit == "degC"


# --- cold-excursion/1 ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _probe_points(rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], policy: ColdPolicy) -> tuple[
        list[ColdEvidence], dict[UUID, str], dict[datetime, str]]:
    """(qualifying points ordered by source time, excluded/context reason by evidence ID, reason by instant)."""
    reasons: dict[UUID, str] = {}
    by_time: dict[datetime, list[ColdEvidence]] = {}
    instant_reason: dict[datetime, str] = {}
    for e in rows:
        if not _is_probe_candidate(e, policy):
            continue
        reason = _exclusion(e, PROBE_ROLE, policy.probe_asset_id)
        if reason is None and not isinstance(e.value, Decimal):
            reason = "UNAVAILABLE"
        if reason is not None:
            reasons[e.evidence_id] = reason
            instant_reason.setdefault(e.source_time, reason)
            continue
        by_time.setdefault(e.source_time, []).append(e)
    points: list[ColdEvidence] = []
    for t, group in sorted(by_time.items()):
        if len({g.value for g in group}) > 1:
            for g in group:
                reasons[g.evidence_id] = "CONTRADICTORY"
            instant_reason[t] = "CONTRADICTORY"
            continue
        points.append(group[0])
        for g in group[1:]:
            reasons[g.evidence_id] = "DUPLICATE"
    return points, reasons, instant_reason


def _gaps(window: ColdWindow, points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], instant_reason: Mapping[datetime, str]
          ) -> tuple[ColdGap, ...]:
    present = {p.source_time for p in points}
    gaps: list[ColdGap] = []
    run: list[datetime] = []
    reason_of = lambda t: instant_reason.get(t, "MISSING_PROBE") if instant_reason.get(t) in GAP_REASONS \
        else "MISSING_PROBE"

    def close(end: datetime) -> None:
        if run:
            gaps.append(ColdGap(run[0], end, reason_of(run[0]), len(run),
                                sum(1 for t in run if t in instant_reason)))
            run.clear()

    for t in window.expected_instants():
        if t in present:
            close(t)
            continue
        if run and reason_of(run[0]) != reason_of(t):
            close(t)
        run.append(t)
    close(window.to_at)
    return tuple(gaps)


def _band(value: Decimal, policy: ColdPolicy) -> str:
    if value > policy.policy_high:
        return "HIGH"
    if value < policy.policy_low:
        return "LOW"
    return "IN"


def _returned(value: Decimal, direction: str, policy: ColdPolicy) -> bool:
    if direction == "HIGH":
        return policy.policy_low <= value <= policy.policy_high - policy.return_hysteresis
    return policy.policy_low + policy.return_hysteresis <= value <= policy.policy_high


def _exceedance(value: Decimal, direction: str, policy: ColdPolicy) -> Decimal:
    over = value - policy.policy_high if direction == "HIGH" else policy.policy_low - value
    return over if over > 0 else Decimal(0)


def _episodes(points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], policy: ColdPolicy) -> list[dict]:
    """Confirmed excursions as index spans over `points`, plus whether any out-of-band sample stayed unconfirmed."""
    found: list[dict] = []
    i, n = 0, len(points)
    while i < n:
        direction = _band(points[i].value, policy)
        if direction == "IN":
            i += 1
            continue
        nxt = points[i + 1] if i + 1 < n else None
        confirmed = (nxt is not None and _band(nxt.value, policy) == direction
                     and SLOT <= nxt.source_time - points[i].source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE)
        if not confirmed:
            found.append({"start": i, "direction": direction, "confirmed": False})
            i += 1
            continue
        j = i + 1
        end_return = None
        while j < n:
            a = points[j]
            b = points[j + 1] if j + 1 < n else None
            if _returned(a.value, direction, policy) and b is not None and _returned(b.value, direction, policy):
                end_return = j
                break
            j += 1
        found.append({"start": i, "direction": direction, "confirmed": True, "return": end_return})
        if end_return is None:
            break
        i = end_return + 2
    return found


def _excursion(points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], span: dict, policy: ColdPolicy, window: ColdWindow,
               rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], reasons: Mapping[UUID, str], claim_ceiling: str,
               gate: SourceGate) -> ColdExcursion:
    i, direction, r = span["start"], span["direction"], span["return"]
    first = points[i]
    before = points[i - 1] if i > 0 else None
    start_latest = first.source_time
    start_earliest = (before.source_time if before is not None and _band(before.value, policy) == "IN"
                      and first.source_time - before.source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE else None)
    last_out_index = (r - 1) if r is not None else len(points) - 1
    # Continuously observed run from the latest start: no step longer than ten minutes is bridged.
    k = i
    while k < last_out_index and points[k + 1].source_time - points[k].source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE:
        k += 1
    continuous_to = points[k].source_time
    if r is not None:
        end_earliest = points[r - 1].source_time
        stable = points[r]
        end_latest = stable.source_time if stable.source_time - end_earliest <= MAX_BRIDGE else None
        observed_end = end_earliest if k == last_out_index else continuous_to
        duration_low = max(Decimal(0), _minutes(observed_end - start_latest))
        duration_high = (_minutes(end_latest - start_earliest)
                         if end_latest is not None and start_earliest is not None else None)
        last_index = r
        is_open = False
    else:
        end_earliest = end_latest = None
        duration_low = max(Decimal(0), _minutes(continuous_to - start_latest))
        duration_high = None
        last_index = len(points) - 1
        is_open = True
    lo_index = i - 1 if start_earliest is not None else i
    dm_low = dm_high = Decimal(0)
    for a, b in zip(points[lo_index:last_index], points[lo_index + 1:last_index + 1]):
        step = b.source_time - a.source_time
        if step > MAX_BRIDGE:
            continue
        ea, eb = _exceedance(a.value, direction, policy), _exceedance(b.value, direction, policy)
        dm_low += min(ea, eb) * _minutes(step)
        dm_high += max(ea, eb) * _minutes(step)
    inside = points[i:last_out_index + 1]
    peak = (max(p.value for p in inside) if direction == "HIGH" else min(p.value for p in inside)) if inside \
        else None
    long_enough = duration_low >= policy.min_excursion_minutes
    if claim_ceiling in ("UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY"):
        claim, exposure = claim_ceiling, False
    elif long_enough and gate.permitted:
        claim, exposure = "BOUNDED", True
    else:
        claim, exposure = "POSSIBLE", False
    span_from = start_earliest or start_latest
    span_to = end_latest or window.to_at
    used = {p.evidence_id for p in points[lo_index:last_index + 1]}
    links: list[ColdLink] = [ColdLink(e, "SUPPORT") for e in sorted(used, key=str)]
    for e in rows:
        if e.evidence_id in used or not (span_from <= e.source_time <= span_to):
            continue
        if e.evidence_id in reasons:
            links.append(ColdLink(e.evidence_id, _use(reasons[e.evidence_id]), reasons[e.evidence_id]))
        elif e.role == DOOR_ROLE:
            links.append(ColdLink(e.evidence_id, "CONTEXT", "DOOR_EVENT"))
    limitation = _excursion_limitation(start_earliest, is_open, long_enough, gate, claim_ceiling)
    return ColdExcursion(direction, first.evidence_id, start_latest, start_earliest, start_latest, end_earliest,
                         end_latest, is_open, duration_low, duration_high, dm_low, dm_high, peak, claim, exposure,
                         tuple(links), limitation)


def _excursion_limitation(start_earliest, is_open, long_enough, gate, ceiling) -> str | None:
    parts = []
    if ceiling == "UNKNOWN":
        parts.append("Probe coverage is below 80 percent of the expected readings, so the excursion is not assessed.")
    if ceiling == "CONTRADICTORY":
        parts.append("Contradictory probe readings block an excursion claim.")
    if start_earliest is None:
        parts.append("The earliest start is not known.")
    if is_open:
        parts.append("The excursion had not returned to the band by the end of the window.")
    if not long_enough and ceiling not in ("UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY"):
        parts.append("The observed duration is shorter than the minimum excursion.")
    if not gate.permitted:
        parts.append("The source classification does not permit a Finding.")
    return " ".join(parts) or None


def assess_excursion(evidence: Iterable[ColdEvidence], window: ColdWindow, policy: ColdPolicy,
                     source_gate: SourceGate) -> ColdAssessment:
    """`cold-excursion/1` over the accepted records known at `window.known_at`."""
    rows = _known(evidence, window)
    points, reasons, instant_reason = _probe_points(rows, policy)
    expected = window.expected_instants()
    known = len({p.source_time for p in points} & set(expected))
    coverage = min(Decimal(1), Decimal(known) / Decimal(len(expected))) if expected else Decimal(0)
    contradictory = any(r == "CONTRADICTORY" for r in reasons.values())
    ceiling = "CONTRADICTORY" if contradictory else ("UNKNOWN" if coverage < COVERAGE_NEEDED else "BOUNDED")
    spans = _episodes(points, policy)
    excursions = tuple(_excursion(points, s, policy, window, rows, reasons, ceiling, source_gate)
                       for s in spans if s["confirmed"])
    unconfirmed = any(not s["confirmed"] for s in spans)
    if ceiling == "CONTRADICTORY":
        state, claim = "CONTRADICTORY", "CONTRADICTORY"
    elif ceiling == "UNKNOWN":
        state, claim = "UNKNOWN", "UNKNOWN"
    elif excursions:
        state = "OBSERVED_EXCURSION"
        claim = "BOUNDED" if any(x.claim_level == "BOUNDED" for x in excursions) else "POSSIBLE"
    elif unconfirmed:
        state, claim = "UNKNOWN", "POSSIBLE"
    else:
        state, claim = "NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED", "BOUNDED"
    links = [ColdLink(p.evidence_id, "SUPPORT") for p in points]
    links += [ColdLink(e, _use(r), r) for e, r in reasons.items()]
    links += [ColdLink(e.evidence_id, "CONTEXT", "DOOR_EVENT") for e in rows if e.role == DOOR_ROLE]
    limitations = []
    if coverage < COVERAGE_NEEDED:
        limitations.append(f"{known} of {len(expected)} expected probe readings are usable.")
    if unconfirmed and not excursions:
        limitations.append("An out-of-band reading was not confirmed by a second reading.")
    return ColdAssessment(EXCURSION_ANALYTIC, MODEL_VERSION, window, state, claim, known, len(expected), coverage,
                          _gaps(window, points, instant_reason), excursions, (), tuple(links), tuple(limitations))


# --- cold-start-response/1 -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _qualifying(rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], role: str, asset_id: UUID | None) -> tuple[
        list[ColdEvidence], dict[UUID, str]]:
    good, reasons = [], {}
    for e in rows:
        if e.role != role and not (e.role is None and e.record_kind in _ROLE_EVENT_KINDS.get(role, ())):
            continue
        reason = _exclusion(e, role, asset_id)
        if reason is None:
            good.append(e)
        else:
            reasons[e.evidence_id] = reason
    return good, reasons


def _first_state(rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], after: datetime | None, values: frozenset | None = None
                 ) -> ColdEvidence | None:
    if after is None:
        return None
    for e in rows:
        if e.source_time >= after - ORDER_TOLERANCE and (values is None or e.value in values):
            return e
    return None


def _chain(correlation: str, group: list[ColdEvidence], rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], policy: ColdStartPolicy,
           window: ColdWindow, gate: SourceGate, probe_rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence]) -> ColdStartChain:
    reasons: dict[UUID, str] = {}
    starts, r = _qualifying(group, START_ROLE, policy.generator_asset_id)
    reasons.update(r)
    grid, r = _qualifying(group, GRID_EVENT_ROLE, None)
    reasons.update(r)
    transfers, r = _qualifying(group, TRANSFER_ROLE, None)
    reasons.update(r)
    requests = [e for e in starts if e.record_kind == "START_REQUEST"]
    request = requests[0] if requests else None
    results = [e for e in starts if e.record_kind == "START_RESULT"
               and (request is None or e.source_time >= request.source_time - ORDER_TOLERANCE)]
    succeeded = [e for e in results if e.outcome == "SUCCEEDED"]
    failed = [e for e in results if e.outcome == "FAILED"]
    losses = [e for e in grid if e.record_kind == "POWER_LOSS"]
    gen_states, r = _qualifying(rows, GENERATOR_STATE_ROLE, policy.generator_asset_id)
    reasons.update(r)
    bus_states, _ = _qualifying(rows, BUS_ROLE, None)
    comp_states, _ = _qualifying(rows, COMPRESSOR_ROLE, None)
    timeout = timedelta(seconds=int(policy.start_timeout_minutes * 60))

    steps: list[ColdStep] = []
    steps.append(ColdStep("GRID", "OBSERVED", losses[0].source_time, (losses[0].evidence_id,)) if losses
                 else ColdStep("GRID", "MISSING", None))
    steps.append(ColdStep("START_REQUEST", "OBSERVED", request.source_time, (request.evidence_id,)) if request
                 else ColdStep("START_REQUEST", "MISSING", None))
    contradictory = bool(succeeded and failed)
    inferred_timeout = False
    if contradictory:
        steps.append(ColdStep("START_RESULT", "CONTRADICTORY", None,
                              tuple(e.evidence_id for e in succeeded + failed)))
        for e in succeeded + failed:
            reasons[e.evidence_id] = "CONTRADICTORY"
        result_at = None
    elif results:
        steps.append(ColdStep("START_RESULT", "OBSERVED", results[0].source_time, (results[0].evidence_id,)))
        result_at = results[0].source_time
    else:
        steps.append(ColdStep("START_RESULT", "MISSING", None))
        result_at = None
        if request is not None and request.source_time + timeout < window.to_at:
            need = [t for t in window.expected_instants()
                    if request.source_time <= t <= request.source_time + timeout]
            seen = {e.source_time: e.value for e in gen_states}
            inferred_timeout = bool(need) and all(t in seen and seen[t] != "RUNNING" for t in need)
    gen_after = result_at if result_at is not None else (request.source_time + timeout if inferred_timeout else None)
    generator = _first_state(gen_states, gen_after, frozenset({"RUNNING", "FAILED"}))
    steps.append(ColdStep("GENERATOR", "OBSERVED", generator.source_time, (generator.evidence_id,)) if generator
                 else ColdStep("GENERATOR", "MISSING", None))
    # The outage window ends at the first restoration: a transfer back to the grid is not the start response.
    restored = [e.source_time for e in grid if e.record_kind == "POWER_RESTORED"]
    in_outage = [e for e in transfers if not restored or e.source_time < restored[0] - ORDER_TOLERANCE]
    transfer = in_outage[0] if in_outage else None
    if policy.transfer_bound:
        steps.append(ColdStep("TRANSFER", "OBSERVED", transfer.source_time, (transfer.evidence_id,)) if transfer
                     else ColdStep("TRANSFER", "MISSING", None))
    anchor_after = (transfer.source_time if transfer else
                    (generator.source_time if generator else result_at))
    bus = _first_state(bus_states, anchor_after)
    steps.append(ColdStep("BUS", "OBSERVED", bus.source_time, (bus.evidence_id,)) if bus
                 else ColdStep("BUS", "MISSING", None))
    compressor = _first_state(comp_states, bus.source_time if bus else None)
    steps.append(ColdStep("COMPRESSOR", "OBSERVED", compressor.source_time, (compressor.evidence_id,))
                 if compressor else ColdStep("COMPRESSOR", "MISSING", None))
    probe = _first_state(probe_rows, compressor.source_time if compressor else None)
    steps.append(ColdStep("PROBE", "OBSERVED", probe.source_time, (probe.evidence_id,)) if probe
                 else ColdStep("PROBE", "MISSING", None))
    missing = tuple(s.step for s in steps if s.state == "MISSING")

    if contradictory:
        result, claim = "CONTRADICTORY", "CONTRADICTORY"
    elif request is None:
        result, claim = "UNKNOWN", "UNKNOWN"
    elif failed:
        result, claim = "OBSERVED_FAILURE", "OBSERVED"
    elif succeeded:
        result, claim = "OBSERVED_SUCCESS", "OBSERVED"
    elif inferred_timeout:
        result, claim = "OBSERVED_FAILURE", "OBSERVED"
    else:
        result, claim = "UNKNOWN", "UNKNOWN"
    support = {i for s in steps if s.state == "OBSERVED" for i in s.evidence_ids}
    links = [ColdLink(i, "SUPPORT") for i in sorted(support, key=str)]
    links += [ColdLink(i, _use(reason), reason) for i, reason in reasons.items() if i not in support
              and any(e.evidence_id == i for e in group)]
    links += [ColdLink(e.evidence_id, "CONTEXT", e.record_kind) for e in group
              if e.evidence_id not in support and e.evidence_id not in reasons]
    anchor = request or min(group, key=lambda e: (e.source_time, str(e.evidence_id)))
    limitation_parts = []
    if missing:
        limitation_parts.append("Not observed: " + ", ".join(missing) + ".")
    if contradictory:
        limitation_parts.append("Contradictory start results block a start-response claim.")
    if result == "OBSERVED_FAILURE" and not gate.permitted:
        limitation_parts.append("The source classification does not permit a Finding.")
    return ColdStartChain(correlation, policy.generator_asset_id, anchor.evidence_id, anchor.source_time,
                          tuple(steps), missing, result, claim, policy.start_timeout_minutes,
                          result == "OBSERVED_FAILURE" and gate.permitted, tuple(links),
                          " ".join(limitation_parts) or None)


def assess_start_response(evidence: Iterable[ColdEvidence], window: ColdWindow, policy: ColdStartPolicy,
                          source_gate: SourceGate) -> ColdAssessment:
    """`cold-start-response/1`: one chain per accepted outage correlation that carries generator start events."""
    rows = _known(evidence, window)
    groups: dict[str, list[ColdEvidence]] = {}
    for e in rows:
        if e.family == "EVENT" and e.correlation_id is not None:
            groups.setdefault(e.correlation_id, []).append(e)
    probe_rows = [e for e in rows if e.role == PROBE_ROLE and e.family == "MEASUREMENT"
                  and _exclusion(e, PROBE_ROLE, policy.probe_asset_id) is None and isinstance(e.value, Decimal)]
    chains = tuple(
        _chain(c, g, rows, policy, window, source_gate, probe_rows)
        for c, g in sorted(groups.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[1][0].source_time, kv[0]))
        if any(e.record_kind in ("START_REQUEST", "START_RESULT") for e in g)
    )
    if not chains:
        state, claim = "UNKNOWN", "UNKNOWN"
    elif any(c.result == "CONTRADICTORY" for c in chains):
        state, claim = "CONTRADICTORY", "CONTRADICTORY"
    else:
        state = chains[-1].result
        claim = chains[-1].claim_level
    links = tuple(link for c in chains for link in c.links)
    return ColdAssessment(START_RESPONSE_ANALYTIC, MODEL_VERSION, window, state, claim, 0, 0, Decimal(0), (), (),
                          chains, links, tuple(c.limitation for c in chains if c.limitation))

"""W2-03: cold assessment episodes and the cold_chain Finding domain.

`.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md` "Migration 0022" and `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` section 2
(`D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`).

Adds three scoped, append-only tables:

- `cold_episode`: one stable EXCURSION or START_RESPONSE episode, identified by `episode_key_sha256`
  (SHA-256 of the canonical `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,anchor evidence id]` key), with an
  optional `split_from_episode_id`. `finding_id` stays NULL in this append-only slice: the Finding links
  to its episode through its immutable claim and exact episode revision, never by a later UPDATE.
- `cold_episode_revision`: an immutable bounded result per assessment basis (claim level, ordered
  start/end bounds, nonnegative ordered duration and degree-minute bounds, peak observed, coverage and
  missing steps).
- `cold_episode_evidence`: exact accepted evidence linked as SUPPORT, CONTEXT or EXCLUDED.

Widens the shared Finding tables from `0009_finding_store` for `cold_chain`: `finding.subject_asset_id`
(scoped asset FK), `finding.generator_asset_id` (text, kept) loses NOT NULL, a domain CHECK keeps the
mini-grid shape, and `finding_revision` gains a stored `domain` (old rows backfilled `mini_grid`) whose
parent FK is `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)`. Episode bounds and the three candidate-minute
fields become nullable for `cold_chain` only; mini-grid revisions keep both bounds and all three minute
fields required. `recompute_job.domain` is text and needs no change.

Downgrade refuses while any cold episode, revision, evidence link or cold Finding exists; otherwise it
restores the old Finding constraints and columns and drops only the objects this revision created. No
row is deleted to force a downgrade.

Revision ID: 0022_w2_cold_assessment
Revises: 0021_w2_cold_config
Create Date: 2026-10-18

COLD_SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE cold_episode (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    kind text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('EXCURSION', 'START_RESPONSE')),
    asset_id uuid NOT NULL,
    anchor_time timestamptz NOT NULL,
    episode_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (episode_key_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    split_from_episode_id uuid NULL,
    finding_id uuid NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_site_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id) REFERENCES site(tenant_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_asset_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, asset_id)
        REFERENCES asset(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_split_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, split_from_episode_id)
        REFERENCES cold_episode(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_finding_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)
        REFERENCES finding(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_key_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, episode_key_sha256),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_not_own_split CHECK (split_from_episode_id IS NULL OR split_from_episode_id <> id)
);
CREATE INDEX cold_episode_site_kind_anchor ON cold_episode (site_id, kind, anchor_time);

CREATE TABLE cold_episode_revision (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    episode_id uuid NOT NULL,
    revision_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (revision_no > 0),
    assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL CHECK (basis_sha256 ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    claim_level text NOT NULL CHECK (claim_level IN ('OBSERVED', 'BOUNDED', 'POSSIBLE', 'UNKNOWN', 'CONTRADICTORY')),
    lower_start timestamptz NULL,
    upper_start timestamptz NULL,
    lower_end timestamptz NULL,
    upper_end timestamptz NULL,
    duration_low numeric NULL,
    duration_high numeric NULL,
    degree_minutes_low numeric NULL,
    degree_minutes_high numeric NULL,
    peak_observed numeric NULL,
    coverage numeric(5, 4) NOT NULL CHECK (coverage BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
    missing_steps jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_episode_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, episode_id)
        REFERENCES cold_episode(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_assessment_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, assessment_revision_id)
        REFERENCES assessment_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_no_unique UNIQUE (episode_id, revision_no),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_basis_unique UNIQUE (episode_id, basis_sha256),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_scope_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_start_order CHECK (
        lower_start IS NULL OR upper_start IS NULL OR lower_start <= upper_start),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_end_order CHECK (
        lower_end IS NULL OR upper_end IS NULL OR lower_end <= upper_end),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_duration CHECK (
        (duration_low IS NULL OR duration_low >= 0) AND (duration_high IS NULL OR duration_high >= 0)
        AND (duration_low IS NULL OR duration_high IS NULL OR duration_low <= duration_high)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_degree_minutes CHECK (
        (degree_minutes_low IS NULL OR degree_minutes_low >= 0)
        AND (degree_minutes_high IS NULL OR degree_minutes_high >= 0)
        AND (degree_minutes_low IS NULL OR degree_minutes_high IS NULL OR degree_minutes_low <= degree_minutes_high)),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_missing_steps_array CHECK (jsonb_typeof(missing_steps) = 'array'),
    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_revision_peak_needs_point CHECK (peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0)
);
CREATE INDEX cold_episode_revision_site_episode ON cold_episode_revision (site_id, episode_id, revision_no DESC);

CREATE TABLE cold_episode_evidence (
    episode_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    evidence_id uuid NOT NULL,
    use text NOT NULL CHECK (use IN ('SUPPORT', 'CONTEXT', 'EXCLUDED')),

    CONSTRAINT cold_episode_evidence_evidence_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, evidence_id)

);

 succeeded in 1344ms:
CREATE INDEX financial_consequence_work_finding
    ON CONFLICT (finding_revision_id) WHERE cause = 'FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED' DO NOTHING;
    RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

-- An accepted generator-power mean may change the technical input of every Finding whose latest revision's
-- episode overlaps it. Coarse on purpose: the worker rechecks and may no-op, but no possibly affected Finding is
-- omitted. Zero-width readings and zero-width episodes overlap nothing (half-open).
CREATE FUNCTION financial_consequence_dirty_on_evidence() RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE
    ev record;
BEGIN
    SELECT e.role, e.interval_start, e.interval_end INTO ev FROM accepted_evidence e
    WHERE e.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND e.site_id = NEW.site_id AND e.id = NEW.accepted_evidence_id;
    IF NOT FOUND OR ev.role <> 'generator_power' OR ev.interval_start IS NULL OR ev.interval_end IS NULL
            OR ev.interval_start >= ev.interval_end THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;
    {ENQUEUE_LOCK}
    INSERT INTO financial_consequence_work (tenant_id, site_id, cause, accepted_evidence_id, finding_id,
                                            finding_revision_id, affected_from, affected_to)
    SELECT NEW.tenant_id, NEW.site_id, 'EVIDENCE_ACCEPTED', NEW.accepted_evidence_id, r.finding_id, r.id,
           ev.interval_start, ev.interval_end
    FROM finding_revision r
    WHERE r.tenant_id = NEW.tenant_id AND r.site_id = NEW.site_id
      AND r.revision_no = (SELECT max(x.revision_no) FROM finding_revision x WHERE x.tenant_id = r.tenant_id
                           AND x.site_id = r.site_id AND x.finding_id = r.finding_id)
      AND r.episode_start < r.episode_end
      AND r.episode_start < ev.interval_end AND r.episode_end > ev.interval_start
    ORDER BY r.episode_start, r.id
    ON CONFLICT (accepted_evidence_id, finding_revision_id) WHERE cause = 'EVIDENCE_ACCEPTED' DO NOTHING;

 succeeded in 1508ms:
"""PostgreSQL Finding store (S4-01 phase 1, migration 0009).

The fifth product database writer (architecture guard rule 8): it only INSERTs
its own append-only tables. Writes for one Site run in one transaction under a
Site-scoped advisory lock and re-check the selection/job fence the matcher read
under, so a selection that lands between the read and the write is never
half-applied: the caller re-plans instead. Revisions are append-only and immutable
(`finding_revision_no_unique`); replay is idempotent against the Finding's latest revision only
(`_insert_revision`), so a basis may return (A -> B -> A, migration 0015) and append a new revision.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Iterable
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection, Engine
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError, ProgrammingError

from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_inputs import sha256_json
from assetops_backend.analytics.dispatch import CANDIDATE, INDETERMINATE, NECESSARY
from assetops_backend.analytics.finding_episode import (
    DOMAIN,
    MATCHER_VERSION,
    MECHANISM,
    TITLES,
    Episode,
    Write,
)


def _num(x) -> str:
    return format(float(x), ".4f")


class FindingStoreUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


class FindingFenceMoved(RuntimeError):
    """A selection or recompute job changed between the matcher's read and its write."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Fence:
    selection_seq: int
    job_count: int
    job_marker: str
    finding_revisions: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Applied:
    finding_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    revision_ids: tuple[str, ...]


class PostgresFindingStore:
    def __init__(self, database_url: str | None) -> None:
        self._database_url = database_url
        self._engine: Engine | None = None

    def _connect(self) -> Engine:
        if not self._database_url:
            raise FindingStoreUnavailable("DATABASE_URL is not set, so the Finding store cannot be reached.")
        if self._engine is None:
            self._engine = create_engine(self._database_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
        return self._engine

    def _read(self, work):
        try:
            with self._connect().connect().execution_options(isolation_level="REPEATABLE READ") as c:
                with c.begin():
                    return work(c)
        except (OperationalError, ProgrammingError) as error:
            raise FindingStoreUnavailable(f"The Finding store failed: {error.orig}") from error

    @staticmethod
    def _fence(c: Connection, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str) -> Fence:
        seq = c.execute(text("SELECT coalesce(max(seq), 0) FROM analytic_run_selection WHERE tenant_id = :t "
                             "AND site_id = :s AND analytic_id = :a"),
                        {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "a": analytic_id}).scalar_one()
        count, marker = c.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*), coalesce(max(created_at)::text, '') || '/' || "
            "coalesce(string_agg(status, ',' ORDER BY id), '') FROM recompute_job WHERE tenant_id = :t "
            "AND site_id = :s"), {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).one()
        # Finding state is part of the fence: a pass planned before another pass committed (whose episodes
        # are stale) must re-plan instead of opening a second Finding for the same episode. Revisions are
        # append-only, so their count only grows.
        revisions = c.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM finding_revision WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s"),
                              {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).scalar_one()
        return Fence(int(seq), int(count), marker, int(revisions))

    def fence(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str) -> Fence:
        return self._read(lambda c: self._fence(c, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id))

    def assessed_days(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str) -> list[date]:
        rows = self._read(lambda c: c.execute(text(
            "SELECT DISTINCT window_start FROM analytic_run_selection WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
            "AND analytic_id = :a ORDER BY window_start"), {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "a": analytic_id}).all())
        return [r[0].date() for r in rows]

    def episodes(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> list[Episode]:
        rows = self._read(lambda c: c.execute(text(
            "SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.id) f.id, f.generator_asset_id, f.anchor_start, r.lifecycle, r.episode_start, "
            "r.episode_end, r.basis_sha256, r.model_version, r.support_sha256, "
            "(SELECT o.claim -> 'recurrence' FROM finding_revision o WHERE o.finding_id = f.id "
            "ORDER BY o.revision_no LIMIT 1) FROM finding f "
            "JOIN finding_revision r "
            "ON r.finding_id = f.id WHERE f.tenant_id = :t AND f.site_id = :s ORDER BY f.id, r.revision_no DESC"),
            {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id}).all())
        return [Episode(str(r[0]), r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5], r[6], r[7], r[8], r[9]) for r in rows]

    def apply(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, analytic_id: str, writes: list[Write], fence: Fence,
              now: datetime, *, deferred: Iterable[datetime] = ()) -> Applied:
        def work(c: Connection) -> Applied:
            c.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('finding:' || :s))"), {"s": str(site_id)})
            if self._fence(c, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id) != fence:
                raise FindingFenceMoved("a selection, recompute job or Finding changed since the matcher read; "
                                        "re-plan")
            findings, revisions = [], []
            for w in writes:
                if w.new_finding is not None:
                    self._insert_finding(c, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, w, now)
                    findings.append(w.finding_id)
                rid = self._insert_revision(c, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, w, now)
                if rid is not None:
                    revisions.append(rid)
            # Q18: the pass itself is recorded, so "no Finding" can cite a completed match at that selection.
            # The windows it deferred (a group touching a missing or non-CURRENT window within the continuity
            # margin) are recorded on it: the pass did not match them, so `match_state` keeps them pending.
            c.execute(text("INSERT INTO finding_match_pass (id, tenant_id, site_id, analytic_id, selection_seq, "
                           "matcher_version, revisions_written, completed_at, deferred_window_starts) VALUES "
                           "(:id, :t, :s, :a, :seq, :matcher, :n, :now, CAST(:deferred AS timestamptz[]))"),
                      {"id": uuid4(), "t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "a": analytic_id, "seq": fence.selection_seq,
                       "matcher": MATCHER_VERSION, "n": len(revisions), "now": now,
                       "deferred": sorted(deferred)})
            return Applied(tuple(findings), tuple(revisions))

        try:
            with self._connect().begin() as c:
                return work(c)
        except (OperationalError, ProgrammingError) as error:
            raise FindingStoreUnavailable(f"The Finding store failed: {error.orig}") from error

    status: str  # DONE | RETRY | FAILED | LEASE_LOST        """One Site's bounded S3 stage (S4-02d): reconcile this Site (a never-assessed Site has no heads and
        enqueues nothing), then process at most `limit` of its due jobs, leased only from this Site."""
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("A Site stage processes at least one job.")
        self.reconcile(tenant_id, site_id)
        outcomes = []
        for _ in range(limit):
            outcome = self.run_once(tenant_id, site_id)
            if outcome is None:
                break
            outcomes.append(outcome)
        return outcomes

    def reconcile_all(self) -> list[Window]:
        scheduled: list[Window] = []
        for tenant_id, site_id in self.store.assessed_sites(self.model.ANALYTIC_ID):
            scheduled += self.reconcile(tenant_id, site_id)
        return scheduled

    def reconcile(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> list[Window]:
        """Enqueue windows whose latest default run used another model version or a configuration
        the current selection no longer gives (backdated Foundation/binding publication)."""
        now = self.clock()
        scheduled = []
        for head in self.store.run_heads(tenant_id, site_id, self.model.ANALYTIC_ID):
            stale = head.model_version != self.model.MODEL_VERSION
            if not stale:
                config = self.store.configuration(tenant_id, site_id, head.window.widened(BRACKET), now)
                stale = configuration_sha256(config) != head.configuration_sha256
            if stale:
                self.store.enqueue(tenant_id, site_id, DOMAIN, head.window, now)
                scheduled.append(head.window)
        return scheduled

def _known(evidence: Iterable[ColdEvidence], window: ColdWindow) -> list[ColdEvidence]:
    rows = [e for e in evidence if window.from_at <= e.source_time < window.to_at
            and e.received_at <= window.known_at and e.decided_at <= window.known_at]
    return sorted(rows, key=lambda e: (e.source_time, e.received_at, str(e.evidence_id)))


def _exclusion(e: ColdEvidence, role: str, asset_id: UUID | None) -> str | None:
    """Why a record of `role` cannot support a claim, or None when it qualifies."""
    if e.role is None or (asset_id is not None and e.asset_id != asset_id):
        return "UNBOUND"
    if not e.clock_attested:
        return "CLOCK_UNATTESTED"
    if e.quality == "SUSPECT":
        return "SUSPECT"
    if e.quality != "GOOD":
        return e.quality
    return None


def _use(reason: str) -> str:
    return "CONTEXT" if reason == "SUSPECT" else "EXCLUDED"


def _minutes(delta: timedelta) -> Decimal:
    return Decimal(int(delta.total_seconds())) / Decimal(60)


def _is_probe_candidate(e: ColdEvidence, policy: ColdPolicy) -> bool:
    if e.family != "MEASUREMENT":
        return False
    if e.role == PROBE_ROLE:
        return True
    # An unbound temperature reading is still a probe-shaped record the gap must name.
    return e.role is None and e.unit == "degC"


# --- cold-excursion/1 ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _probe_points(rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], policy: ColdPolicy) -> tuple[
        list[ColdEvidence], dict[UUID, str], dict[datetime, str]]:
    """(qualifying points ordered by source time, excluded/context reason by evidence ID, reason by instant)."""
    reasons: dict[UUID, str] = {}
    by_time: dict[datetime, list[ColdEvidence]] = {}
    instant_reason: dict[datetime, str] = {}
    for e in rows:
        if not _is_probe_candidate(e, policy):
            continue
        reason = _exclusion(e, PROBE_ROLE, policy.probe_asset_id)
        if reason is None and not isinstance(e.value, Decimal):
            reason = "UNAVAILABLE"
        if reason is not None:
            reasons[e.evidence_id] = reason
            instant_reason.setdefault(e.source_time, reason)
            continue
        by_time.setdefault(e.source_time, []).append(e)
    points: list[ColdEvidence] = []
    for t, group in sorted(by_time.items()):
        if len({g.value for g in group}) > 1:
            for g in group:
                reasons[g.evidence_id] = "CONTRADICTORY"
            instant_reason[t] = "CONTRADICTORY"
            continue
        points.append(group[0])
        for g in group[1:]:
            reasons[g.evidence_id] = "DUPLICATE"
    return points, reasons, instant_reason


def _gaps(window: ColdWindow, points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], instant_reason: Mapping[datetime, str]
          ) -> tuple[ColdGap, ...]:
    present = {p.source_time for p in points}
    gaps: list[ColdGap] = []
    run: list[datetime] = []
    reason_of = lambda t: instant_reason.get(t, "MISSING_PROBE") if instant_reason.get(t) in GAP_REASONS \
        else "MISSING_PROBE"

    def close(end: datetime) -> None:
        if run:
            gaps.append(ColdGap(run[0], end, reason_of(run[0]), len(run),
                                sum(1 for t in run if t in instant_reason)))
            run.clear()

    for t in window.expected_instants():
        if t in present:
            close(t)
            continue
        if run and reason_of(run[0]) != reason_of(t):
            close(t)
        run.append(t)
    close(window.to_at)
    return tuple(gaps)


def _band(value: Decimal, policy: ColdPolicy) -> str:
    if value > policy.policy_high:
        return "HIGH"
    if value < policy.policy_low:
        return "LOW"
    return "IN"


def _returned(value: Decimal, direction: str, policy: ColdPolicy) -> bool:
    if direction == "HIGH":
        return policy.policy_low <= value <= policy.policy_high - policy.return_hysteresis
    return policy.policy_low + policy.return_hysteresis <= value <= policy.policy_high


def _exceedance(value: Decimal, direction: str, policy: ColdPolicy) -> Decimal:
    over = value - policy.policy_high if direction == "HIGH" else policy.policy_low - value
    return over if over > 0 else Decimal(0)


def _episodes(points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], policy: ColdPolicy) -> list[dict]:
    """Confirmed excursions as index spans over `points`, plus whether any out-of-band sample stayed unconfirmed."""
    found: list[dict] = []
    i, n = 0, len(points)
    while i < n:
        direction = _band(points[i].value, policy)
        if direction == "IN":
            i += 1
            continue
        nxt = points[i + 1] if i + 1 < n else None
        confirmed = (nxt is not None and _band(nxt.value, policy) == direction
                     and SLOT <= nxt.source_time - points[i].source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE)
        if not confirmed:
            found.append({"start": i, "direction": direction, "confirmed": False})
            i += 1
            continue
        j = i + 1
        end_return = None
        while j < n:
            a = points[j]
            b = points[j + 1] if j + 1 < n else None
            if _returned(a.value, direction, policy) and b is not None and _returned(b.value, direction, policy):
                end_return = j
                break
            j += 1
        found.append({"start": i, "direction": direction, "confirmed": True, "return": end_return})
        if end_return is None:
            break
        i = end_return + 2
    return found


def _excursion(points: Sequence[ColdEvidence], span: dict, policy: ColdPolicy, window: ColdWindow,
               rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], reasons: Mapping[UUID, str], claim_ceiling: str,
               gate: SourceGate) -> ColdExcursion:
    i, direction, r = span["start"], span["direction"], span["return"]
    first = points[i]
    before = points[i - 1] if i > 0 else None
    start_latest = first.source_time
    start_earliest = (before.source_time if before is not None and _band(before.value, policy) == "IN"
                      and first.source_time - before.source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE else None)
    last_out_index = (r - 1) if r is not None else len(points) - 1
    # Continuously observed run from the latest start: no step longer than ten minutes is bridged.
    k = i
    while k < last_out_index and points[k + 1].source_time - points[k].source_time <= MAX_BRIDGE:
        k += 1
    continuous_to = points[k].source_time
    if r is not None:
        end_earliest = points[r - 1].source_time
        stable = points[r]
        end_latest = stable.source_time if stable.source_time - end_earliest <= MAX_BRIDGE else None
        observed_end = end_earliest if k == last_out_index else continuous_to
        duration_low = max(Decimal(0), _minutes(observed_end - start_latest))
        duration_high = (_minutes(end_latest - start_earliest)
                         if end_latest is not None and start_earliest is not None else None)
        last_index = r
        is_open = False
    else:
        end_earliest = end_latest = None
        duration_low = max(Decimal(0), _minutes(continuous_to - start_latest))
        duration_high = None
        last_index = len(points) - 1
        is_open = True
    lo_index = i - 1 if start_earliest is not None else i
    dm_low = dm_high = Decimal(0)
    for a, b in zip(points[lo_index:last_index], points[lo_index + 1:last_index + 1]):
        step = b.source_time - a.source_time
        if step > MAX_BRIDGE:
            continue
        ea, eb = _exceedance(a.value, direction, policy), _exceedance(b.value, direction, policy)
        dm_low += min(ea, eb) * _minutes(step)
        dm_high += max(ea, eb) * _minutes(step)
    inside = points[i:last_out_index + 1]
    peak = (max(p.value for p in inside) if direction == "HIGH" else min(p.value for p in inside)) if inside \
        else None
    long_enough = duration_low >= policy.min_excursion_minutes
    if claim_ceiling in ("UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY"):
        claim, exposure = claim_ceiling, False
    elif long_enough and gate.permitted:
        claim, exposure = "BOUNDED", True
    else:
        claim, exposure = "POSSIBLE", False
    span_from = start_earliest or start_latest
    span_to = end_latest or window.to_at
    used = {p.evidence_id for p in points[lo_index:last_index + 1]}
    links: list[ColdLink] = [ColdLink(e, "SUPPORT") for e in sorted(used, key=str)]
    for e in rows:
        if e.evidence_id in used or not (span_from <= e.source_time <= span_to):
            continue
        if e.evidence_id in reasons:
            links.append(ColdLink(e.evidence_id, _use(reasons[e.evidence_id]), reasons[e.evidence_id]))
        elif e.role == DOOR_ROLE:
            links.append(ColdLink(e.evidence_id, "CONTEXT", "DOOR_EVENT"))
    limitation = _excursion_limitation(start_earliest, is_open, long_enough, gate, claim_ceiling)
    return ColdExcursion(direction, first.evidence_id, start_latest, start_earliest, start_latest, end_earliest,
                         end_latest, is_open, duration_low, duration_high, dm_low, dm_high, peak, claim, exposure,
                         tuple(links), limitation)


def _excursion_limitation(start_earliest, is_open, long_enough, gate, ceiling) -> str | None:
    parts = []
    if ceiling == "UNKNOWN":
        parts.append("Probe coverage is below 80 percent of the expected readings, so the excursion is not assessed.")
    if ceiling == "CONTRADICTORY":
        parts.append("Contradictory probe readings block an excursion claim.")
    if start_earliest is None:
        parts.append("The earliest start is not known.")
    if is_open:
        parts.append("The excursion had not returned to the band by the end of the window.")
    if not long_enough and ceiling not in ("UNKNOWN", "CONTRADICTORY"):
        parts.append("The observed duration is shorter than the minimum excursion.")
    if not gate.permitted:
        parts.append("The source classification does not permit a Finding.")
    return " ".join(parts) or None


def assess_excursion(evidence: Iterable[ColdEvidence], window: ColdWindow, policy: ColdPolicy,
                     source_gate: SourceGate) -> ColdAssessment:
    """`cold-excursion/1` over the accepted records known at `window.known_at`."""
    rows = _known(evidence, window)
    points, reasons, instant_reason = _probe_points(rows, policy)
    expected = window.expected_instants()
    known = len({p.source_time for p in points} & set(expected))
    coverage = min(Decimal(1), Decimal(known) / Decimal(len(expected))) if expected else Decimal(0)
    contradictory = any(r == "CONTRADICTORY" for r in reasons.values())
    ceiling = "CONTRADICTORY" if contradictory else ("UNKNOWN" if coverage < COVERAGE_NEEDED else "BOUNDED")
    spans = _episodes(points, policy)
    excursions = tuple(_excursion(points, s, policy, window, rows, reasons, ceiling, source_gate)
                       for s in spans if s["confirmed"])
    unconfirmed = any(not s["confirmed"] for s in spans)
    if ceiling == "CONTRADICTORY":
        state, claim = "CONTRADICTORY", "CONTRADICTORY"
    elif ceiling == "UNKNOWN":
        state, claim = "UNKNOWN", "UNKNOWN"
    elif excursions:
        state = "OBSERVED_EXCURSION"
        claim = "BOUNDED" if any(x.claim_level == "BOUNDED" for x in excursions) else "POSSIBLE"
    elif unconfirmed:
        state, claim = "UNKNOWN", "POSSIBLE"
    else:
        state, claim = "NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED", "BOUNDED"
    links = [ColdLink(p.evidence_id, "SUPPORT") for p in points]
    links += [ColdLink(e, _use(r), r) for e, r in reasons.items()]
    links += [ColdLink(e.evidence_id, "CONTEXT", "DOOR_EVENT") for e in rows if e.role == DOOR_ROLE]
    limitations = []
    if coverage < COVERAGE_NEEDED:
        limitations.append(f"{known} of {len(expected)} expected probe readings are usable.")
    if unconfirmed and not excursions:
        limitations.append("An out-of-band reading was not confirmed by a second reading.")
    return ColdAssessment(EXCURSION_ANALYTIC, MODEL_VERSION, window, state, claim, known, len(expected), coverage,
                          _gaps(window, points, instant_reason), excursions, (), tuple(links), tuple(limitations))

def _qualifying(rows: Sequence[ColdEvidence], role: str, asset_id: UUID | None) -> tuple[

 succeeded in 881ms:
"""Site-scoped exact Finding read (S4-01 phase 2) at the `A-claim-work-routes` API routes.

`D-2026-10-09-pre-s3-claim-routes-and-actor`: the Site code resolves inside the configured tenant; an
unauthenticated caller is 401 and an authenticated caller without the known Site scope is 403 (the `access`
seam decides both); a foreign or unknown Site, Finding or revision is 404; an unavailable store is a typed
503, never an empty claim. No human sign-in exists yet: the default composition admits the development
tenant's operator context exactly as the S3 Performance read does, and says so in every body.

Q19 (`D-2026-10-12-s4-finding-phase-2-read-boundaries`): the S3 Performance cutover gate (comparison,
lineage breaks, other-model heads) guards both routes. While it is closed a default/current read is a typed
409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED`, never a positive Finding or `NO_FINDING`; a pinned `revision_id` is served
as non-current audit material (`SOURCE_CLOSED`), and S3 drill-down links are withheld with the reason.
An unconfigured gate is closed.

S4-03 phase 1 (`I-finding-evidence-read`, `I-finding-history-read`) adds two child reads of the same root,
`.../findings/{finding_id}/evidence?revision_id=&offset=&limit=` and
`.../history?revision_id=&after_revision_no=&limit=`,
with the same scope, gate and refusal semantics; a broken lineage join is a named 200 state, a store failure 503.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from typing import Any, Callable
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query, Request
from fastapi.encoders import jsonable_encoder

from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_ports import AssessmentStoreUnavailable
from assetops_backend.analytics.finding_read import FindingReadService
from assetops_backend.analytics.ports import AssessmentStoreUnavailable as FileAssessmentStoreUnavailable
from assetops_backend.evidence.ports import EvidenceStoreUnavailable

Scope = Callable[[str], "tuple[str, UUID, UUID] | None"]
# (request, site_code) -> None to admit, else 401 or 403.
Access = Callable[[Request, str], "int | None"]
ACCESS_BASIS = ("DEVELOPMENT_TENANT_OPERATOR_CONTEXT: no authenticated human principal exists yet; the read is "
                "admitted for the configured development tenant, as the S3 Performance read is")


Gate = Callable[[str, UUID, UUID], "tuple[bool, str]"]
CLOSED_LINKS = "S3 Performance drill-down routes answer 409 PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED while the gate is closed"


def development_access(request: Request, site_code: str) -> int | None:
    return None


def no_gate(site_code: str, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    return False, "No Performance cutover gate is configured for the Finding read."


def build_finding_router(reads: FindingReadService | None, scope: Scope, unavailable: tuple[type, ...] = (),
                         access: Access = development_access, access_basis: str = ACCESS_BASIS,
                         gate: Gate = no_gate, consequences: Any = None,
                         consequence_unavailable: tuple[type, ...] = ()) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter()
    down = (AssessmentStoreUnavailable, FileAssessmentStoreUnavailable, EvidenceStoreUnavailable, *unavailable)
    consequence_down = (*down, *consequence_unavailable)

    def open_scope(request: Request, site_code: str) -> tuple[str, UUID, UUID]:
        refusal = access(request, site_code)
        if refusal == 401:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
                                detail={"code": "UNAUTHENTICATED", "message": "Sign in to read Findings."})
        if refusal == 403:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail={"code": "SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN",
                                                         "message": f"No access to Site {site_code!r}."})
        found = scope(site_code)
        if found is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No imported Site {site_code!r} in this tenant.")
        if reads is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail={"code": "FINDING_STORE_UNAVAILABLE",
                                                         "message": "No product database is configured."})
        return found

    def source_gate(code: str, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            permitted, reason = gate(code, tenant_id, site_id)
        except down as error:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail={"code": "FINDING_STORE_UNAVAILABLE",
                                                         "message": str(error)}) from error
        return {"permitted": permitted, "reason": reason}

    def closed(source: dict[str, Any]) -> HTTPException:
        return HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={
            "code": "PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED", "read": "CLOSED", "message": source["reason"],
            "note": "The Finding's authority is the selected S3 assessment, which is closed for this Site; no "
                    "current Finding or no-Finding verdict is shown. An exact revision_id reads audit material."})

    def served(work: Callable[[], Any], missing: str) -> Any:
        try:
            body = work()
        except down as error:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail={"code": "FINDING_STORE_UNAVAILABLE",
                                                         "message": str(error)}) from error
        if body is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=missing)
        return jsonable_encoder(body)

    @router.get("/api/sites/{site_code}/finding-windows/{day}")
    def window_state(request: Request, site_code: str, day: date) -> dict[str, Any]:
        code, tenant_id, site_id = open_scope(request, site_code)
        source = source_gate(code, tenant_id, site_id)

    END IF;
END;

function Location() {
  const location = useLocation();
  return <output data-testid="location">{location.pathname + location.search}</output>;
}

function renderCold(path: string, read: (at: string | null) => ColdFoundationRead, health: EvidenceRead<GatewayHealth> = healthOf(["edge-cold-001"])) {
  const cold: ColdFoundationClient = { getColdFoundation: vi.fn((_site: string, at: string | null) => Promise.resolve(read(at))) };
  const gatewayHealth: GatewayHealthClient = { getGatewayHealth: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve(health)) };
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
      <App
        flags={featureFlagsWith(false)}
        siteDirectory={directory}
        siteDetail={detail}
        siteAnalytics={EMPTY_ANALYTICS_CLIENT}
        siteFoundation={genericFoundation}
        coldFoundation={cold}
        siteEvidence={{ ...createSiteEvidenceClient(), gatewayHealth }}
      />
      <Location />
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
  return cold;
}

const loaded = (value: ColdFoundationReadModel) => (): ColdFoundationRead => ({ status: "loaded", value });
const BASE = "/sites/COLD-001/foundation";
const gaps = () => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-panel-gap]"));
const main = () => document.querySelector("main") as HTMLElement;

describe("W2-01: cold Foundation routes keep the v69 panel structure", () => {
  it.each([
    ["cold-foundation", "", {}],
    ["cold-readiness", "/readiness", { gateway: "edge-cold-001" }],
    ["cold-sld", "/sld", { site: "Kobo vaccine cold store" }],
  ] as const)("stocked COLD-001: %s", async (screenId, segment, values) => {
    renderCold(`${BASE}${segment}`, loaded(STOCKED));
    await settledScreen();

    expect(document.querySelector("[data-page-refusal]")).toBeNull();

  ] as const)("missing probe and no contact (COLD-002): %s", async (screenId, segment, values) => {

  });

 Tests: `backend/tests/test_w203_cold_rules.py::{test_in_band_no_excursion,test_two_high_points_and_open_bounds,test_no_gap_bridge,test_start_success_and_failure,test_missing_transfer_and_probe,test_contradictory_good_outcomes,test_suspect_clock_skew_unbound_excluded}`; `backend/tests/test_w203_cold_migration_postgres.py::{test_upgrade_preserves_minigrid_and_constraints,test_scoped_fk_and_append_only,test_duplicate_episode_basis,test_downgrade_refuses_live_cold}`; `backend/tests/test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py::{test_all_six_w202_manifests_through_intake,test_late_recompute_keeps_history,test_same_basis_idempotent,test_split_from_invalidation,test_worker_retry_atomic,test_equal_event_id_conflict_not_support}`; `backend/tests/test_w203_cold_reads_postgres.py::{test_window_limit_and_paging,test_typed_errors_and_unknown_200,test_tenant_source_gate,test_pending_historical_reload}`; `frontend/src/sites/__tests__/coldRoutes.test.tsx::{temperature,coldbackup,cold-finding,cold-finding-excursion}` exact panel/layout/gap cases.
