+Commits: (1) `0022` plus migration/guard probes; (2) pure cold rules and replay vectors; (3) transactional worker/episode/Finding writes and late recompute; (4) selected read API; (5) four routes/panel/capture evidence and packet. Each commit has focused proof; full suite after (5).
+
+## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.
+
+## Commits 3-5 exact contract
+
+**Commit 3 — transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
+
+Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.
+
+The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10–19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15–19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.
+
+**Commit 4 — selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.
+
+`temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. Filter every Finding query by `domain='cold_chain'`, tenant, Site and selected window; existing mini-grid Finding reads remain unchanged.
+
+**Commit 5 — exact v69 slots.** Route COLDCHAIN Site Performance/Temperature to `temperature`, Performance/Backup to `coldbackup`, and cold Finding detail by `kind` to `cold-finding` for START_RESPONSE or `cold-finding-excursion` for EXCURSION. The four panel titles below are the indexed titles in `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`; retain their DOM order, grid/prominence and shared W1-F1 layout guard even when pending, empty or refused. No leading app-only answer card.

+| Screen | Exact numbered panels and data placement |
+| --- | --- |
+| `temperature` | 1 **Temperature with operating context**: selected probe points, policy band, sampled chart and explicit gaps; 2 **Excursion context**: episode start/end/duration/degree-minute bounds and observed peak with exact Finding link only if persisted; 3 **Claim boundary**: coverage, exclusions, source gate and plain limits including no spoilage determination. |
+| `coldbackup` | 1 **What the chain shows**: selected result/claim and missing/contradictory link; 2 **Reconstructed event chain**: eight ordered steps and evidence links; 3 **Backup event chains**: correlation-scoped list and observed/unknown status; 4 **Evidence by causal step**: exact accepted IDs, quality and excluded/context reasons. No Incident or part diagnosis. |
+| `cold-finding` | 1 **Bounded conclusion**: selected start Finding result, claim and limits; 2 **Operational context**: chain, missing steps, episode/revision history and evidence/Analysis links; 3 **Recommended next step**: `No recorded next step` until a real Action/Recommendation record exists. |
+| `cold-finding-excursion` | 1 **Bounded conclusion**: selected excursion Finding, start/end/duration bounds and no spoilage claim; 2 **Temperature**: sampled probe/policy/gaps, observed peak, degree-minute bounds and exact evidence links; 3 **Recommended next step**: `No recorded next step` until a real linked record exists. |

Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
+
+Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.

+The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10–19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15–19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.

+**Commit 4 — selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.

Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. +Commits: (1) `0022` plus migration/guard probes; (2) pure cold rules and replay vectors; (3) transactional worker/episode/Finding writes and late recompute; (4) selected read API; (5) four routes/panel/capture evidence and packet. Each commit has focused proof; full suite after (5).
+
+## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

+## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.
+
+## Commits 3-5 exact contract
+
+**Commit 3 — transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
+
+Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.
+
+The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10–19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15–19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.
+
+**Commit 4 — selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.
+
+`temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. +## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. _NONNEGATIVE = ("cold-door-heat", "cold-compressor-start-delay", "cold-probe-error", "cold-return-hysteresis")

#: Readiness wording. The first four are the design's verbatim publication-readiness messages.
MESSAGE_PROBE = "Add a chamber temperature probe and bind its Â°C reading"
MESSAGE_POLICY = "Set a lower and upper temperature policy for this room"
MESSAGE_START = "Bind the generator start request and result"
MESSAGE_TRANSFER = "Transfer position is not reported; switch state is unknown"
MESSAGE_NO_ROOM = "Declare a cold room"
CONTACT_UNVERIFIED = "Contact time cannot be verified"
# D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact: why a successful read has no contact time. Each maps to NEVER with as_of null
# and names the absent health read or gateway match; none is a claim about equipment or physical non-contact.
CONTACT_HEALTH_ABSENT = "No gateway health read is configured"
CONTACT_NO_GATEWAY_REGISTERED = "No gateway is registered for this site"
CONTACT_NO_GATEWAY_SELECTED = "No gateway is selected for this site"
CONTACT_NO_DIAGNOSTIC = "No diagnostic is recorded for the selected gateway"

READY = "READY_TO_REPORT"
INCOMPLETE = "CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE"


# --- Foundation validation (refuses, before anything is written) ----------------------------------------------


def validate_cold_foundation(foundation: SiteFoundation, *, source: str,
                             invalid: Callable[[str], NoReturn]) -> None:
    """Refuse a COLDCHAIN Foundation that breaks a section 3 rule. Runs after the shared strict parser."""
    types = {c.component_id: c.component_type for c in foundation.components}
    for component in foundation.components:
        values: dict[str, float] = {}
        for prop in component.properties or ():
            carrier = COLD_PROPERTY_CARRIERS.get(prop.property_key)
            if carrier is None:
                continue
            if component.component_type != carrier:
                invalid(f"{prop.property_key} belongs on a {carrier} component, not on {component.component_id} "
                        f"({component.component_type}), in {source}.")
            values[prop.property_key] = prop.value
        for key in _STRICTLY_POSITIVE:
            if key in values and not values[key] > 0:
                invalid(f"{key} on {component.component_id} must be greater than zero in {source}.")
        for key in _NONNEGATIVE:
            if key in values and values[key] < 0:
                invalid(f"{key} on {component.component_id} must not be negative in {source}.")
        for band in ("policy", "control"):
            low, high = values.get(f"cold-{band}-low"), values.get(f"cold-{band}-high")
            if (low is None) != (high is None):
                invalid(f"{component.component_id} declares only one end of its {band} band in {source}; "
                        "a band needs both a lower and an upper temperature.")
            if low is not None and high is not None and not low < high:
                invalid(f"{component.component_id}'s {band} band low ({low:g} degC) must be below its high "
                        f"({high:g} degC) in {source}.")
        hysteresis = values.get("cold-return-hysteresis")
        if hysteresis is not None:
            low, high = values.get("cold-control-low"), values.get("cold-control-high")
            if low is None or high is None:
                invalid(f"{component.component_id} declares a return hysteresis without a control band in {source}.")
            if not hysteresis < high - low:
                invalid(f"{component.component_id}'s return hysteresis ({hysteresis:g} degC) must be smaller than "
                        f"its control band width ({high - low:g} degC) in {source}.")
    if foundation.topology is not None:
        _validate_cold_topology(foundation, types, source=source, invalid=invalid)


def _validate_cold_topology(foundation: SiteFoundation, types: Mapping[str, str], *, source: str,
                            invalid: Callable[[str], NoReturn]) -> None:
    topology = foundation.topology
    assert topology is not None
    node_type: dict[str, str] = {}
    for node in topology.nodes:
        component_type = types[node.component_id]

+## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.
+

Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
+
+Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.
+
+The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10–19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15–19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.

+**Commit 4 — selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. -Commits: (1) `0022` plus migration/guard probes; (2) pure cold rules and replay vectors; (3) transactional worker/episode/Finding writes and late recompute; (4) selected read API; (5) four routes/panel/capture evidence and packet. Each commit has focused proof; full suite after (5).
+Commits: (1) `0022` plus migration/guard probes; (2) pure cold rules and replay vectors; (3) transactional worker/episode/Finding writes and late recompute; (4) selected read API; (5) four routes/panel/capture evidence and packet. Each commit has focused proof; full suite after (5).
+
+## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)
+
+1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, then restores the original unconditional trigger because the old schema has no `domain`. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
+2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
+3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
+4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
+5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
+6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
+7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
+8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
+9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
+10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
+11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.
+
+## Commits 3-5 exact contract
+
+**Commit 3 — transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
+
+Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.
+
+The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10–19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15–19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.
+
+**Commit 4 — selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.
+
+`temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. Filter every Finding query by `domain='cold_chain'`, tenant, Site and selected window; existing mini-grid Finding reads remain unchanged.
+
+**Commit 5 — exact v69 slots.** Route COLDCHAIN Site Performance/Temperature to `temperature`, Performance/Backup to `coldbackup`, and cold Finding detail by `kind` to `cold-finding` for START_RESPONSE or `cold-finding-excursion` for EXCURSION. The four panel titles below are the indexed titles in `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`; retain their DOM order, grid/prominence and shared W1-F1 layout guard even when pending, empty or refused. No leading app-only answer card.

+| --- | --- |
+| `temperature` | 1 **Temperature with operating context**: selected probe points, policy band, sampled chart and explicit gaps; 2 **Excursion context**: episode start/end/duration/degree-minute bounds and observed peak with exact Finding link only if persisted; 3 **Claim boundary**: coverage, exclusions, source gate and plain limits including no spoilage determination. |
+| `coldbackup` | 1 **What the chain shows**: selected result/claim and missing/contradictory link; 2 **Reconstructed event chain**: eight ordered steps and evidence links; 3 **Backup event chains**: correlation-scoped list and observed/unknown status; 4 **Evidence by causal step**: exact accepted IDs, quality and excluded/context reasons. No Incident or part diagnosis. |
+| `cold-finding` | 1 **Bounded conclusion**: selected start Finding result, claim and limits; 2 **Operational context**: chain, missing steps, episode/revision history and evidence/Analysis links; 3 **Recommended next step**: `No recorded next step` until a real Action/Recommendation record exists. |
+| `cold-finding-excursion` | 1 **Bounded conclusion**: selected excursion Finding, start/end/duration bounds and no spoilage claim; 2 **Temperature**: sampled probe/policy/gaps, observed peak, degree-minute bounds and exact evidence links; 3 **Recommended next step**: `No recorded next step` until a real linked record exists. |
